CREATE TABLE `rapor_tahfidz` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `id_santri` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `kelas` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `judul_materi` varchar(100) DEFAULT 'Materi Tahfidz',
  `semester` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `tahun_ajaran` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `juz` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_ustadz` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `fk_rapor_header_santri` (`id_santri`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_rapor_header_santri` FOREIGN KEY (`id_santri`) REFERENCES `santri` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

===== pages\wali\pr.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya untuk Role Orang Tua (ortu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom status_ortu di tabel `pr` jika belum ada
$cek_kolom = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM pr LIKE 'status_ortu'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE pr ADD COLUMN status_ortu ENUM('Belum','Selesai') DEFAULT 'Belum' AFTER deskripsi");
}

// Ambil daftar anak yang terhubung dengan akun orang tua ini
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

// Proses Toggle Status PR ("Sudah Disimak di Rumah" / "Belum")
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['toggle_status_pr']) && $santri) {
    $id_pr = (int) $_POST['id_pr'];
    $status_baru = ($_POST['status_sekarang'] === 'Selesai') ? 'Belum' : 'Selesai';
    
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE pr SET status_ortu = '$status_baru' WHERE id = $id_pr AND id_santri = {$santri['id']}");
    $_SESSION['success'] = ($status_baru === 'Selesai') 
        ? "Alhamdulillah, tugas rumah ditandai sudah disimak di rumah!" 
        : "Status tugas rumah dikembalikan menjadi belum disimak.";
    
    header("Location: pr_anak.php?id_santri=" . $santri['id']);
    exit;
}

$pr_list = [];
$total_pr = 0;
$pr_selesai = 0;
$pr_belum = 0;

if ($santri) {
    $q = "SELECT p.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
          FROM pr p 
          LEFT JOIN users u ON p.id_ustadz = u.id 
          WHERE p.id_santri = {$santri['id']} 
          ORDER BY FIELD(p.status_ortu, 'Belum', 'Selesai'), p.tanggal DESC, p.id DESC";
    $pr_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $q), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

    $total_pr = count($pr_list);
    foreach ($pr_list as $item) {
        if (($item['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum') === 'Selesai') {
            $pr_selesai++;
        } else {
            $pr_belum++;
        }
    }
}

$persen_selesai = $total_pr > 0 ? round(($pr_selesai / $total_pr) * 100) : 0;

$Nama_Hari_Indo = [
    'Sunday' => 'Minggu', 'Monday' => 'Senin', 'Tuesday' => 'Selasa',
    'Wednesday' => 'Rabu', 'Thursday' => 'Kamis', 'Friday' => 'Jumat', 'Saturday' => 'Sabtu'
];
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Tugas Rumah (PR) Ananda - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header Halaman -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-orange-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="clipboard-list" class="w-7 h-7 text-orange-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Tugas Rumah (PR) Ananda
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Dampingi ananda menyelesaikan tugas hafalan atau muraja'ah mandiri di rumah.</p>
            </div>

            <?php if (count($anak_list) > 1): ?>
            <form method="GET" action="pr_anak.php" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                <label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Pilih Ananda:</label>
                <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php foreach ($anak_list as $a): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $a['id'] ?>" <?= $a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>>
                            <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama']) ?>
                        </option>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                </select>
            </form>
            <?php endif; ?>
        </div>

        <!-- Notifikasi Sukses -->
        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['success'])): ?>
            <div class="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl mb-6 text-sm flex items-center gap-3 shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0"></i>
                <span class="font-medium"><?= $_SESSION['success']; unset($_SESSION['success']); ?></span>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (!$santri): ?>
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border-l-4 border-amber-500 text-amber-800 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-sm">
                Data santri belum terhubung dengan akun orang tua Anda. Silakan hubungi Ustadz/Ustadzah pembimbing.
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>

        <!-- RINGKASAN PROFIL & STATISTIK PR -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-8">
            
            <!-- Kartu Profil & Progress -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-4">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                        <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-orange-500 to-amber-600 text-white font-extrabold text-lg flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
                            <?= strtoupper(substr($santri['nama'], 0, 1)) ?>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900"><?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nama']) ?></h2>
                            <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nis'] ?: '-') ?> • Progres Pendampingan Rumah</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="text-xl font-black text-orange-600 bg-orange-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl border border-orange-200">
                        <?= $persen_selesai ?>%
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <div class="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 mb-1.5">
                        <div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: <?= $persen_selesai ?>%"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                        <span>Disimak Ortu: <b class="text-emerald-600"><?= $pr_selesai ?> Tugas</b></span>
                        <span>Total Tugas: <b class="text-slate-700"><?= $total_pr ?> Tugas</b></span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Belum Disimak -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-amber-200 border-b-4 border-b-amber-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Perlu Disimak</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $pr_belum ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Tugas aktif di rumah</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="clock" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Sudah Disimak -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-emerald-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Sudah Disimak</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $pr_selesai ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Selesai didampingi</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>

        <!-- TOOLBAR FILTER & PENCARIAN -->
        <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 mb-6 shadow-sm flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button type="button" onclick="setFilterPR('ALL', this)" class="tab-filter bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                    Semua Tugas (<?= $total_pr ?>)
                </button>
                <button type="button" onclick="setFilterPR('Belum', this)" class="tab-filter bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                    ⏳ Belum Disimak (<?= $pr_belum ?>)
                </button>
                <button type="button" onclick="setFilterPR('Selesai', this)" class="tab-filter bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                    ✅ Sudah Disimak (<?= $pr_selesai ?>)
                </button>
            </div>

            <div class="relative w-full sm:w-64">
                <i data-lucide="search" class="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5"></i>
                <input type="text" id="cariPR" oninput="terapkanFilterPR()" placeholder="Cari judul atau ayat PR..." 
                    class="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500 outline-none">
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- DAFTAR KARTU PR -->
        <?php if (empty($pr_list)): ?>
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-12 text-center shadow-sm">
                <div class="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                    <i data-lucide="sparkles" class="w-8 h-8"></i>
                </div>
                <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">Belum Ada Tugas Rumah</h3>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Saat ini belum ada PR yang diberikan oleh Ustadz/Ustadzah pembimbing.</p>
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6" id="gridPR">
                <?php foreach ($pr_list as $p): 
                    $status = $p['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum';
                    $is_selesai = ($status === 'Selesai');
                    $is_baru = (!$is_selesai && strtotime($p['tanggal']) >= strtotime('-2 days'));
                    
                    $hari_en = date('l', strtotime($p['tanggal']));
                    $hari_indo = $Nama_Hari_Indo[$hari_en] ?? $hari_en;
                ?>
                <div class="kartu-pr bg-white border <?= $is_selesai ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/10' : ($is_baru ? 'border-orange-300 ring-2 ring-orange-100' : 'border-slate-200') ?> rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between transition hover:shadow-md"
                     data-status="<?= $status ?>"
                     data-search="<?= strtolower(htmlspecialchars($p['judul'] . ' ' . $p['deskripsi'])) ?>">
                    
                    <div>
                        <!-- Header Kartu -->
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
                            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">
                                <i data-lucide="calendar" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-500"></i>
                                <?= $hari_indo ?>, <?= date('d M Y', strtotime($p['tanggal'])) ?>
                            </span>

                            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <?php if ($is_baru): ?>
                                    <span class="bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                                        Tugas Baru
                                    </span>
                                <?php endif; ?>

                                <?php if ($is_selesai): ?>
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                                        <i data-lucide="check-circle" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Sudah Disimak
                                    </span>
                                <?php else: ?>
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                                        <i data-lucide="clock" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Belum Disimak
                                    </span>
                                <?php endif; ?>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Judul & Instruksi -->
                        <h3 class="text-lg font-extrabold <?= $is_selesai ? 'text-slate-600 line-through decoration-emerald-500/50' : 'text-slate-900' ?> mb-2">
                            <?= htmlspecialchars($p['judul']) ?>
                        </h3>
                        <div class="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-xl text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed mb-5">
                            <?= nl2br(htmlspecialchars($p['deskripsi'])) ?>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Footer Kartu & Tombol Aksi Orang Tua -->
                    <div class="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            <div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                                <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 leading-none">Pemberi Tugas</p>
                                <p class="font-bold text-slate-700 mt-0.5"><?= htmlspecialchars($p['nama_ustadz'] ?? 'Ustadz/ah Pembimbing') ?></p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <form method="POST" class="m-0"><?php echo csrfTokenField(); ?>
                            <input type="hidden" name="toggle_status_pr" value="1">
                            <input type="hidden" name="id_pr" value="<?= $p['id'] ?>">
                            <input type="hidden" name="status_sekarang" value="<?= $status ?>">
                            
                            <?php if ($is_selesai): ?>
                                <button type="submit" class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold transition flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                    <i data-lucide="rotate-ccw" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> Batalkan Tanda
                                </button>
                            <?php else: ?>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

$target_data = null;
$ayat_tercapai_target = 0;
$stat_data = ['total_ziyadah' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0];
$riwayat_list = [];
$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];

if ($santri) {
    $id_santri = $santri['id'];
    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

    // Ambil Target Aktif & Progresnya
    $target_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"));
    if ($target_data) {
        $tgl_mulai = $target_data['tanggal_mulai'];
        $q_capai = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(jumlah_ayat), 0) as capai FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND jenis = 'ziyadah' AND tanggal >= '$tgl_mulai'");
        $ayat_tercapai_target = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_capai)['capai'];
    }

    // Statistik Bulan Ini
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ziyadah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                   FROM hafalan 
                   WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    // Grafik 6 Bulan
    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q = "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q))['z'];
    }

    // Riwayat 20 Setoran Terakhir
    $riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
                      FROM hafalan h 
                      LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id 
                      WHERE h.id_santri = $id_santri 
                      ORDER BY h.tanggal DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 20";
    $riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
}

CREATE TABLE `absensi` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_santri` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `tanggal` date NOT NULL,
  `status` enum('Hadir','Sakit','Izin','Alfa') NOT NULL,
  `keterangan` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `id_ustadz` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp()
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_uca1400_ai_ci;

-- Struktur dari tabel `hafalan`
--

CREATE TABLE `hafalan` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_santri` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `tanggal` date NOT NULL,
  `jenis` enum('ziyadah','murajaah','tasmi') NOT NULL,
  `surat` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `ayat_awal` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `ayat_akhir` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `jumlah_ayat` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `predikat` enum('A','B','C') DEFAULT NULL,
  `catatan` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `id_ustadz` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp()
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_uca1400_ai_ci;

===== pages\absensi\index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya untuk Role Orang Tua (ortu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Ambil daftar anak yang terhubung dengan akun orang tua ini
$anak_query = "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC";
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $anak_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Pilih anak aktif (default: anak pertama)
$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

// Filter Bulan & Tahun (Default: Bulan & Tahun Saat Ini)
$bulan_pilih = isset($_GET['bulan']) ? str_pad((int)$_GET['bulan'], 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT) : date('m');
$tahun_pilih = isset($_GET['tahun']) ? (int) $_GET['tahun'] : (int) date('Y');
$periode     = "$tahun_pilih-$bulan_pilih";

$nama_bulan = [
    '01' => 'Januari', '02' => 'Februari', '03' => 'Maret', '04' => 'April',
    '05' => 'Mei', '06' => 'Juni', '07' => 'Juli', '08' => 'Agustus',
    '09' => 'September', '10' => 'Oktober', '11' => 'November', '12' => 'Desember'
];

$Nama_Hari_Indo = [
    'Sunday' => 'Minggu', 'Monday' => 'Senin', 'Tuesday' => 'Selasa',
    'Wednesday' => 'Rabu', 'Thursday' => 'Kamis', 'Friday' => 'Jumat', 'Saturday' => 'Sabtu'
];

$rekap = ['H' => 0, 'S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];
$absensi_list = [];
$map_tanggal = []; // Untuk kalender visual

if ($santri) {
    $id_santri = (int) $santri['id'];

    // Ambil data absensi dari tabel `absensi` pada periode terpilih
    $q_absensi = "SELECT a.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
                  FROM absensi a 
                  LEFT JOIN users u ON a.id_ustadz = u.id 
                  WHERE a.id_santri = $id_santri 
                    AND DATE_FORMAT(a.tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                  ORDER BY a.tanggal DESC, a.id DESC";
    $res_absensi = mysqli_query($conn, $q_absensi);
    if ($res_absensi) {
        $absensi_list = mysqli_fetch_all($res_absensi, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    foreach ($absensi_list as $row) {
        $inisial = strtoupper(substr(trim($row['status']), 0, 1));
        if (isset($rekap[$inisial])) {
            $rekap[$inisial]++;
        }
        $tgl_angka = (int) date('j', strtotime($row['tanggal']));
        $map_tanggal[$tgl_angka] = [
            'inisial'    => $inisial,
            'keterangan' => $row['keterangan'] ?? '',
            'ustadz'     => $row['nama_ustadz'] ?? 'Ustadz/ah'
        ];
    }
}

$total_pertemuan = array_sum($rekap);
$persentase_hadir = $total_pertemuan > 0 ? round(($rekap['H'] / $total_pertemuan) * 100) : 0;

// Konfigurasi Kalender Bulan Terpilih
$jumlah_hari_bulan = cal_days_in_month(CAL_GREGORIAN, (int)$bulan_pilih, $tahun_pilih);
$hari_pertama_minggu = (int) date('N', strtotime("$tahun_pilih-$bulan_pilih-01")); // 1 (Senin) s/d 7 (Minggu)
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Kehadiran Anak - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header & Filter Periode -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-blue-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="calendar-check" class="w-7 h-7 text-blue-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Kehadiran & Presensi Ananda
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Pantau rekapitulasi kehadiran harian dan kedisiplinan ananda di Surau Tahfizh Firdaus.</p>
            </div>

            <!-- Filter Anak, Bulan & Tahun -->
            <form method="GET" action="absensi_anak.php" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <?php if (count($anak_list) > 1): ?>
                    <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <?php foreach ($anak_list as $a): ?>
                            <option value="<?= $a['id'] ?>" <?= $a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>>
                                👦 <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama']) ?>
                            </option>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </select>
                <?php endif; ?>

                <select name="bulan" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php foreach ($nama_bulan as $num => $nama_bln): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $num ?>" <?= $num == $bulan_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>><?= $nama_bln ?></option>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                </select>

                <select name="tahun" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php for ($th = date('Y') - 2; $th <= date('Y') + 1; $th++): ?>

                                $cls_box = 'bg-rose-500 text-white border-rose-600 font-bold shadow-sm';
                                $title_tip = "Tgl $tgl: ALFA";

                        echo '<div title="' . htmlspecialchars($title_tip) . '" class="h-10 rounded-xl border flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-default transition ' . $cls_box . '">';

                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-blue-500 inline-block"></span> Sakit (S)</div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-amber-500 inline-block"></span> Izin (I)</div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-rose-500 inline-block"></span> Alfa (A)</div>
                </div>

            <!-- TABEL RINCIAN PRESENSI -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">

                    <div>

                            <i data-lucide="list-checks" class="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600"></i> Rincian Log Kehadiran
                        </h3>
                        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Daftar presensi tercatat pada bulan <?= $nama_bulan[$bulan_pilih] ?> <?= $tahun_pilih ?></p>
                    </div>

                    <select id="filterStatus" onchange="filterLogAbsensi()" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">

                                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Status</th>

                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody class="text-sm divide-y divide-slate-100">
                            <?php if (empty($absensi_list)): ?>
                                <tr>
                                    <td colspan="4" class="py-12 text-center">
                                        <div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-400">

                                            <p class="text-xs mt-1">Pilih bulan lain pada filter di kanan atas untuk melihat riwayat sebelumnya.</p>

                            <?php else: ?>

                                ?>

===== config\database.php =====
<?php
$host = getenv('SIRAH_DB_HOST') ?: 'localhost';
$username = getenv('SIRAH_DB_USER') ?: 'root';
$password = getenv('SIRAH_DB_PASSWORD');
$password = $password === false ? '' : $password;
$database = getenv('SIRAH_DB_NAME') ?: 'hafalan_santri';
$environment = getenv('SIRAH_ENV') ?: 'development';

if ($environment === 'production' && ($username === 'root' || $password === '')) {
    error_log('SIRAH production database credentials are not configured safely.');
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Konfigurasi database produksi belum aman.');
}

mysqli_report(MYSQLI_REPORT_OFF);
$conn = @new mysqli($host, $username, $password, $database);

if ($conn->connect_error) {
    error_log('SIRAH database connection failed: ' . $conn->connect_error);
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Koneksi database gagal. Periksa konfigurasi server.');
}

$conn->set_charset("utf8mb4");

?>

===== pages\pengguna\ustadz.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Operator, Pimpinan, dan Ustadz (dikontrol juga dari hak_akses_menu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom jenis_kelamin di tabel users jika belum ada
$cek_kolom = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'jenis_kelamin'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN jenis_kelamin ENUM('L','P') DEFAULT 'L' AFTER nama_lengkap");
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom id_halaqah di tabel users jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_h = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'id_halaqah'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_h) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN id_halaqah INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER role");
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom nama_pengajar di tabel halaqah jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_np = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM halaqah LIKE 'nama_pengajar'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_np) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE halaqah ADD COLUMN nama_pengajar VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL AFTER id_ustadz");
}

// === 1. PROSES TAMBAH USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_ustadz'])) {
    $nama_lengkap  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $jenis_kelamin = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $username      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $password      = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $nama_lokal    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lokal']));
    $sebutan       = ($jenis_kelamin === 'P') ? 'Ustadzah' : 'Ustadz';

    // Cek apakah Lokal/Halaqah sudah ada, jika belum buat baru
    $cek_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nama_lokal'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_lokal) > 0) {
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_lokal)['id'];
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah) VALUES ('$nama_lokal')");
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    // Cek duplikasi Username
    $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah digunakan! Silakan gunakan username lain.";
    } else {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, jenis_kelamin, role, id_halaqah) 
                VALUES ('$username', '$password', '$nama_lengkap', '$jenis_kelamin', 'ustadz', $id_halaqah)";
        if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
            $id_user = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = $id_user, nama_pengajar = '$nama_lengkap' WHERE id = $id_halaqah");
            $_SESSION['success'] = "$sebutan '$nama_lengkap' berhasil ditambahkan ke $nama_lokal!";
        } else {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal menyimpan data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
        }
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. PROSES EDIT USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['edit_ustadz'])) {
    $id_user       = (int) $_POST['id_user'];
    $nama_lengkap  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $jenis_kelamin = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $username      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $nama_lokal    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lokal']));
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');
    $sebutan       = ($jenis_kelamin === 'P') ? 'Ustadzah' : 'Ustadz';

    // Cek username bentrok dengan user lain
    $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username' AND id != $id_user");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah dipakai pengguna lain!";
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }

    // Cek / Buat Lokal Mengajar
    $cek_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nama_lokal'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_lokal) > 0) {
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_lokal)['id'];
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah) VALUES ('$nama_lokal')");
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    $pass_sql = "";
    if (!empty($password_baru)) {
        $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $pass_sql = ", password = '$hash'";
    }

    $sql_update = "UPDATE users SET 
                    nama_lengkap = '$nama_lengkap',
                    jenis_kelamin = '$jenis_kelamin',
                    username = '$username',
                    id_halaqah = $id_halaqah
                    $pass_sql
                   WHERE id = $id_user AND role = 'ustadz'";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_update)) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = $id_user, nama_pengajar = '$nama_lengkap' WHERE id = $id_halaqah");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data $sebutan '$nama_lengkap' berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal memperbarui data.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. PROSES HAPUS USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus'])) {
    $id = (int) $_POST['hapus'];
    if (!in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan'], true)) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = 'Hanya operator atau pimpinan yang dapat menghapus akun pengajar.';
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = NULL WHERE id_ustadz = $id");
        mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id AND role = 'ustadz'");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data pengajar (Ustadz/Ustadzah) berhasil dihapus.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil data seluruh pengajar beserta nama lokalnya
$query_ustadz = "SELECT u.*, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM users u 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                 WHERE u.role = 'ustadz' 
                 ORDER BY u.nama_lengkap ASC";
$res_ustadz = mysqli_query($conn, $query_ustadz);
$ustadz_list = $res_ustadz ? mysqli_fetch_all($res_ustadz, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

// Hitung Statistik Ustadz & Ustadzah
$total_pengajar = count($ustadz_list);
$total_ustadz   = 0;
$total_ustadzah = 0;
foreach ($ustadz_list as $u) {
    // Deteksi otomatis: jika jenis_kelamin 'P' atau namanya mengandung kata 'ustadzah'
    $is_putri = (($u['jenis_kelamin'] ?? 'L') === 'P' || stripos($u['nama_lengkap'], 'ustadzah') !== false);
    if ($is_putri) {
        $total_ustadzah++;
    } else {
        $total_ustadz++;
    }
}

===== pages\pengguna\profil_saya.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_user = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role    = $_SESSION['role'];

// Ambil data user saat ini
$user_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT u.*, h.nama_halaqah 
                               FROM users u 
                               LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                               WHERE u.id = $id_user LIMIT 1");
$user_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($user_q);

// Jika role ortu, ambil data santri terkait untuk menampilkan/mengedit No. HP WhatsApp
$santri_ortu = null;
if ($role === 'ortu') {
    $s_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_user LIMIT 1");
    $santri_ortu = mysqli_fetch_assoc($s_q);
}

// === 1. PROSES UPDATE PROFIL ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['update_profil'])) {
    $nama_baru   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $username_br = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    
    // Cek apakah username dipakai orang lain
    $cek_u = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_br' AND id != $id_user");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_u) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username_br' sudah digunakan oleh pengguna lain!";
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET nama_lengkap = '$nama_baru', username = '$username_br' WHERE id = $id_user");
        $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] = stripslashes($nama_baru);
        $_SESSION['username']     = stripslashes($username_br);

        // Jika ustadz/ah, sinkronkan juga nama_pengajar di tabel halaqah
        if ($role === 'ustadz') {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET nama_pengajar = '$nama_baru' WHERE id_ustadz = $id_user");
        }

        // Jika ortu, update juga nama_ortu & no_hp_ortu di tabel santri
        if ($role === 'ortu' && isset($_POST['no_hp_ortu'])) {
            $hp_baru = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['no_hp_ortu']));
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET nama_ortu = '$nama_baru', no_hp_ortu = '$hp_baru' WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_user");
        }

        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data profil Anda berhasil diperbarui!";
    }
    header("Location: profil_saya.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. PROSES GANTI PASSWORD ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['ganti_password'])) {
    $pass_lama       = $_POST['password_lama'] ?? '';
    $pass_baru       = $_POST['password_baru'] ?? '';
    $pass_konfirmasi = $_POST['konfirmasi_password'] ?? '';

    if (!password_verify($pass_lama, $user_data['password'])) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Password saat ini yang Anda masukkan salah!";
    } elseif (strlen($pass_baru) < 5) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Password baru minimal harus terdiri dari 5 karakter!";
    } elseif ($pass_baru !== $pass_konfirmasi) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Konfirmasi password baru tidak cocok!";
    } else {
        $hash_baru = password_hash($pass_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET password = '$hash_baru' WHERE id = $id_user");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Password akun Anda berhasil diubah! Gunakan password baru saat login berikutnya.";
    }
    header("Location: profil_saya.php");
    exit;
}

$label_role = ucfirst($role);
if ($role === 'ustadz') {
    $label_role = isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user) ? 'Ustadzah (Pengajar Putri)' : 'Ustadz (Pengajar Putra)';
} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $label_role = 'Wali Santri / Orang Tua';
} elseif ($role === 'operator') {
    $label_role = 'Operator Utama Sistem';
}

===== pages\pengguna\hak_akses.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: HANYA OPERATOR yang boleh mengakses halaman pengaturan hak akses ini
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'operator') {
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

// === 1. SIMPAN PERUBAHAN MATRIKS HAK AKSES MENU ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['simpan_hak_akses'])) {
    $semua_menu = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, kode_menu FROM hak_akses_menu"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

    foreach ($semua_menu as $m) {
        $id_m = (int)$m['id'];
        // Operator selalu memiliki akses penuh (1) ke kelola_hak_akses
        $op   = ($m['kode_menu'] === 'kelola_hak_akses') ? 1 : (isset($_POST['akses_operator'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0);
        $pimp = isset($_POST['akses_pimpinan'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0;
        $ust  = isset($_POST['akses_ustadz'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0;
        $ortu = isset($_POST['akses_ortu'][$id_m]) ? 1 : 0;

        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE hak_akses_menu SET 
                                akses_operator = $op,
                                akses_pimpinan = $pimp,
                                akses_ustadz = $ust,
                                akses_ortu = $ortu
                             WHERE id = $id_m");
    }

    $_SESSION['success'] = "Konfigurasi hak akses menu untuk seluruh Actor berhasil diperbarui secara real-time!";
    header("Location: kelola_hak_akses.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. TAMBAH AKUN ACTOR BARU (OPERATOR / PIMPINAN / USTADZ) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_akun_actor'])) {
    $nama_lengkap = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $username     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $password     = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $role_baru    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['role_baru']);

    $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah terdaftar!";
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('$username', '$password', '$nama_lengkap', '$role_baru')");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Akun '$nama_lengkap' dengan role " . strtoupper($role_baru) . " berhasil dibuat!";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_hak_akses.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. HAPUS AKUN PENGELOLA ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus_user'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int)$_POST['hapus_user'];
    if ($id_hapus !== (int)$_SESSION['user_id']) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id_hapus");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Akun pengguna berhasil dihapus.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Anda tidak dapat menghapus akun Operator yang sedang Anda gunakan login.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_hak_akses.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil seluruh daftar menu dikelompokkan per kategori
$menu_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM hak_akses_menu ORDER BY urutan ASC");
$menu_list = mysqli_fetch_all($menu_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$menu_by_kategori = [];
foreach ($menu_list as $m) {
    $menu_by_kategori[$m['kategori']][] = $m;
}

// Ambil daftar akun Operator & Pimpinan
$admin_users = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM users WHERE role IN ('operator', 'pimpinan') ORDER BY role ASC, nama_lengkap ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Statistik ringkas sistem untuk Operator
$stat_op   = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='operator'"))['c'];
$stat_pimp = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='pimpinan'"))['c'];
$stat_ust  = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='ustadz'"))['c'];
$stat_ortu = (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM users WHERE role='ortu'"))['c'];
?>

$page_title = "Manajemen Hak Akses Actor - Operator Sistem";

===== includes\sidebar.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/init_rbac.php';

$role          = $_SESSION['role'] ?? '';
$nama          = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? 'Pengguna';
$id_user_login = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
$halaman_aktif = basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF']);

// Proteksi Otomatis: Cek apakah role yang sedang login diizinkan mengakses file halaman ini
if (!empty($role) && !cekIzinAksesFile($conn, $role, $halaman_aktif)) {
    $_SESSION['error'] = "Akses ditolak! Role Anda tidak memiliki izin untuk membuka halaman tersebut.";
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

// Penyesuaian label role & warna badge (Deteksi Akurat Ustadz vs Ustadzah dari DB & Lokal)
$label_role = 'Pengguna';
$badge_role_class = 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700';

if ($role === 'operator') {
    $label_role = 'Operator Sistem';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/40';
} elseif ($role === 'ustadz') {
    if (isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user_login)) {
        $label_role = 'Ustadzah';
        $badge_role_class = 'bg-teal-500/15 text-teal-300 border-teal-500/30';
    } else {
        $label_role = 'Ustadz';
        $badge_role_class = 'bg-indigo-500/15 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-500/30';
    }
} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $label_role = 'Wali Santri';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30';
} elseif ($role === 'pimpinan') {
    $label_role = 'Pimpinan';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30';
}

// Ambil daftar menu dari database `hak_akses_menu` sesuai Role yang sedang login
$kolom_akses = [
    'operator' => 'akses_operator',
    'pimpinan' => 'akses_pimpinan',
    'ustadz'   => 'akses_ustadz',
    'ortu'     => 'akses_ortu'
][$role] ?? 'akses_ortu';

$q_menu_sidebar = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM hak_akses_menu WHERE $kolom_akses = 1 ORDER BY urutan ASC");
$sidebar_menus = $q_menu_sidebar ? mysqli_fetch_all($q_menu_sidebar, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

$grouped_sidebar = [];
foreach ($sidebar_menus as $sm) {
    $grouped_sidebar[$sm['kategori']][] = $sm;
}
?>
<aside class="w-64 bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 flex flex-col h-screen fixed left-0 top-0 border-r border-slate-800/80 shadow-2xl z-40 select-none">
    
    <!-- BRAND HEADER -->
    <div class="p-5 border-b border-slate-800/80 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-900 to-slate-950">
        <a href="dashboard.php" class="flex items-center gap-3.5 group">
            <div class="relative w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-indigo-700 p-0.5 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-950/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <div class="w-full h-full bg-slate-950 rounded-[10px] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                    <i data-lucide="book-open-check" class="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400"></i>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="min-w-0">
                <h1 class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white tracking-wide leading-tight group-hover:text-indigo-300 transition">
                    SURAU TAHFIZH
                </h1>
                <p class="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">Mushalla Firdaus</p>
            </div>
        </a>
    </div>

    <!-- DYNAMIC NAVIGATION LINKS BERDASARKAN HAK AKSES ACTOR -->
    <nav class="flex-1 py-5 px-3.5 overflow-y-auto sidebar-scroll space-y-5">
        <?php foreach ($grouped_sidebar as $nama_kategori => $items): ?>
        <div>
            <p class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 px-3">
                <?= htmlspecialchars($nama_kategori) ?>
            </p>
            <ul class="space-y-1">
                <?php foreach ($items as $item): 
                    $terkait_arr = array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(',', $item['file_terkait'] ?? '')));
                    $all_files   = array_merge([$item['file_utama']], $terkait_arr);
                    $aktif       = in_array($halaman_aktif, $all_files);
                    $cls_item    = $aktif 
                        ? 'relative bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600/90 to-indigo-600/70 text-white font-semibold shadow-md shadow-indigo-950/50 border border-indigo-400/20'

                ?>

            <div class="relative shrink-0">

===== includes\init_rbac.php =====
<?php
// Pastikan koneksi database sudah ada
if (!isset($conn)) {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/database.php';
}

// 1. Pastikan ENUM role di tabel `users` mendukung 'operator'
$cek_role_col = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'role'");
if ($cek_role_col) {
    $col_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_role_col);
    if (stripos($col_info['Type'], 'operator') === false) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users MODIFY COLUMN `role` ENUM('operator','pimpinan','ustadz','ortu') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ustadz'");
    }
}

// Pastikan kolom jenis_kelamin & id_halaqah ada di tabel users
$cek_jk_u = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'jenis_kelamin'");
if ($cek_jk_u && mysqli_num_rows($cek_jk_u) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN jenis_kelamin ENUM('L','P') DEFAULT 'L' AFTER nama_lengkap");
}

// 2. Bootstrap akun operator hanya jika password kuat diberikan lewat environment.
$cek_op = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE role = 'operator' LIMIT 1");
$bootstrap_operator_password = getenv('SIRAH_BOOTSTRAP_OPERATOR_PASSWORD');
if ($cek_op && mysqli_num_rows($cek_op) === 0 && is_string($bootstrap_operator_password) && strlen($bootstrap_operator_password) >= 16) {
    $pass_op = password_hash($bootstrap_operator_password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('operator', '$pass_op', 'Operator Utama Sistem', 'operator')");
}
unset($bootstrap_operator_password);

// 3. Buat tabel `hak_akses_menu` untuk mengatur izin menu per aktor secara dinamis
mysqli_query($conn, "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `hak_akses_menu` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `kode_menu` varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  `nama_menu` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `file_utama` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `file_terkait` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `kategori` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `icon` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'circle',
  `warna_icon` varchar(50) DEFAULT 'text-slate-400',
  `urutan` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `akses_operator` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `akses_pimpinan` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `akses_ustadz` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `akses_ortu` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `unik_kode_menu` (`kode_menu`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4");

// 4. Seed (Isi Awal) Daftar Menu seluruh Web jika tabel masih kosong
$cek_isi_menu = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM hak_akses_menu");
$total_menu_db = $cek_isi_menu ? (int)mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_isi_menu)['total'] : 0;

if ($total_menu_db === 0) {
    $default_menus = [
        ['dashboard', 'Dashboard Utama', 'dashboard.php', 'index.php', 'Menu Utama', 'layout-dashboard', 'text-indigo-400', 1, 1, 1, 1, 1],
        ['kelola_hak_akses', 'Hak Akses & Menu Actor', 'kelola_hak_akses.php', '', 'Manajemen Sistem', 'shield-check', 'text-rose-400', 2, 1, 0, 0, 0],
        ['kelola_ustadz', 'Kelola Ustadz & Ustadzah', 'kelola-ustadz.php', 'proses_tambah_ustadz.php,proses_hapus_ustadz.php', 'Manajemen Lembaga', 'users', 'text-indigo-400', 3, 1, 1, 0, 0],
        ['kelola_kelas', 'Kelas / Lokal Halaqah', 'kelola_kelas.php', '', 'Manajemen Lembaga', 'layout-grid', 'text-amber-400', 4, 1, 1, 1, 0],
        ['kelola_santri', 'Kelola Data Santri', 'kelola_santri.php', 'proses_tambah_santri.php,proses_edit_santri.php,proses_hapus_santri.php', 'Manajemen Lembaga', 'graduation-cap', 'text-emerald-400', 5, 1, 1, 1, 0],
        ['target_santri', 'Target Hafalan Santri', 'target_santri.php', 'proses_target_santri.php', 'Manajemen Lembaga', 'target', 'text-teal-400', 6, 1, 1, 1, 0],
        ['input_ziyadah', 'Input Setoran Ziyadah', 'input_ziyadah.php', 'proses_input_hafalan.php', 'Pencatatan Harian', 'book-plus', 'text-emerald-400', 7, 1, 0, 1, 0],

    }

/** Shared application helpers for permissions, santri filters, and notifications. */
// 5. Fungsi Helper Cek Izin Akses File
function cekIzinAksesFile($conn, $role, $nama_file) {
    if ($role === 'operator') return true;
    if (in_array($nama_file, ['login.php', 'proses_login.php', 'logout.php', 'dashboard.php', 'index.php', 'profil_saya.php'])) return true;

    $kolom_role = [
        'pimpinan' => 'akses_pimpinan',
        'ustadz'   => 'akses_ustadz',
        'ortu'     => 'akses_ortu'
    ][$role] ?? '';

    if (empty($kolom_role)) return false;

    $file_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $nama_file);
    $q = "SELECT $kolom_role FROM hak_akses_menu 
          WHERE file_utama = '$file_esc' OR FIND_IN_SET('$file_esc', file_terkait) > 0";
    $res = mysqli_query($conn, $q);

    if ($res && mysqli_num_rows($res) > 0) {
        while ($r = mysqli_fetch_assoc($res)) {
            if ((int)$r[$kolom_role] === 1) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// 6. Fungsi Pintar: Deteksi apakah user yang login adalah Ustadzah (Perempuan) atau Ustadz (Laki-laki)
function isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user) {
    $id_user = (int) $id_user;
    if ($id_user <= 0) return false;

    $q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT u.jenis_kelamin, u.nama_lengkap, u.username, h.nama_halaqah 
                              FROM users u 
                              LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                              WHERE u.id = $id_user LIMIT 1");
    if ($q && $row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q)) {
        // Cek 1: Kolom jenis_kelamin di tabel users = 'P'
        if (($row['jenis_kelamin'] ?? '') === 'P') return true;
        // Cek 2: Username diawali 'ustadzah' atau nama mengandung 'ustadzah'
        if (stripos($row['username'] ?? '', 'ustadzah') !== false) return true;
        if (stripos($row['nama_lengkap'] ?? '', 'ustadzah') !== false) return true;
        // Cek 3: Mengajar di lokal yang namanya mengandung 'Perempuan' atau 'Akhwat'
        if (stripos($row['nama_halaqah'] ?? '', 'perempuan') !== false || stripos($row['nama_halaqah'] ?? '', 'akhwat') !== false) {
            // Sekalian perbaiki kolom jenis_kelamin di database agar permanen 'P'
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET jenis_kelamin = 'P' WHERE id = $id_user");
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

// 7. Fungsi Terpusat: Ambil SQL Filter Santri Khusus Binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah
function getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_user, $role, $alias_tabel_santri = 's', $allow_mode_badal = true) {
    // Operator & Pimpinan bisa melihat seluruh santri
    if (in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan'])) {
        return "";
    }

    // Mode Badal: Ustadz/Ustadzah menampilkan semua santri (?semua=1)
    if ($allow_mode_badal && isset($_GET['semua']) && $_GET['semua'] === '1') {
        return "";
    }

    $id_user = (int) $id_user;
    $u_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap, jenis_kelamin FROM users WHERE id = $id_user"));
    $id_h_user = (int) ($u_info['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nm_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_info['nama_lengkap'] ?? ''));

    // Cari semua ID lokal yang diampu oleh Ustadz/Ustadzah ini
    $q_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah FROM halaqah 
                                    WHERE id_ustadz = $id_user 
                                       OR id = $id_h_user 
                                       OR (nama_pengajar IS NOT NULL AND nama_pengajar != '' AND LOWER(nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nm_esc'))");
    $lokal_ids = [];
    if ($q_lokal) {
        while ($lk = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_lokal)) {
            $lokal_ids[] = (int) $lk['id'];
        }
    }

    $prefix = !empty($alias_tabel_santri) ? "$alias_tabel_santri." : "";

    // Jika ustadz/ustadzah sudah punya lokal binaan, filter HANYA santri di lokal tersebut!
    if (!empty($lokal_ids)) {
        $list_id = implode(',', $lokal_ids);
        return " WHERE {$prefix}id_halaqah IN ($list_id) ";
    }

    // Jika ustadz/ustadzah belum punya lokal spesifik, filter berdasarkan santri yang id_ustadz = beliau ATAU sesuai jenis kelaminnya (Putri utk Ustadzah, Putra utk Ustadz)
    $is_ustadzah = isPengajarUstadzah($conn, $id_user);
    $jk_target   = $is_ustadzah ? 'P' : 'L';
    return " WHERE ({$prefix}id_ustadz = $id_user OR ({$prefix}id_halaqah IS NULL AND {$prefix}jenis_kelamin = '$jk_target')) ";
}

function santriDalamBinaan($conn, $id_santri, $id_user, $role) {
    $id_santri = (int) $id_santri;
    $filter = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, (int) $id_user, $role, 's', false);
    $and_filter = $filter !== '' ? ' AND ' . preg_replace('/^\s*WHERE\s+/i', '', $filter) : '';
    $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT s.id FROM santri s WHERE s.id = ? $and_filter LIMIT 1");
    if (!$stmt) {
        return false;
    }
    mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'i', $id_santri);
    mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
    $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
    $found = $result && mysqli_fetch_assoc($result) !== null;
    mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
    return $found;
}

// 8. Fungsi Helper: Buat Link WhatsApp Otomatis untuk Laporan Setoran & PR ke Orang Tua
function buatLinkWaHafalan($no_hp, $nama_santri, $nama_ortu, $jenis, $tanggal, $juz, $pojok, $surat, $ayat_awal, $ayat_akhir, $predikat, $catatan, $nama_pengajar = 'Ustadz/Ustadzah') {
    $hp = preg_replace('/[^0-9]/', '', $no_hp ?? '');
    if (empty($hp)) return '';
    if (strpos($hp, '0') === 0) {
        $hp = '62' . substr($hp, 1);
    }

    $kat = strtoupper($jenis);
    $tgl_indo = date('d/m/Y', strtotime($tanggal));
    $ortu_sapaan = !empty($nama_ortu) ? "Bapak/Ibu *$nama_ortu*" : "Orang Tua/Wali";
    $info_juz = !empty($juz) ? "Juz $juz" . (!empty($pojok) ? " ($pojok)" : "") : "-";
    $info_ayat = ($jenis === 'tasmi') ? "Ujian Sekali Duduk" : "QS. $surat : Ayat $ayat_awal - $ayat_akhir";
    $ket_predikat = ($predikat === 'A') ? "A (Sangat Baik / Lancar)" : (($predikat === 'B') ? "B (Lancar dengan Catatan)" : "C (Perlu Mengulang)");
    $cat_teks = !empty($catatan) ? $catatan : "Alhamdulillah lancar, mohon terus dimotivasi di rumah.";

    $pesan  = "Assalamu'alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuh,\n";
    $pesan .= "Yth. $ortu_sapaan,\n\n";
    $pesan .= "Berikut laporan setoran *$kat* Ananda di *Surau Tahfizh Firdaus*:\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Nama Santri:* $nama_santri\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Tanggal:* $tgl_indo\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Juz / Pojok:* $info_juz\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Materi:* $info_ayat\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Predikat:* *$ket_predikat*\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Catatan:* _\"$cat_teks\"_\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "Disimak oleh: *$nama_pengajar*\n";
    $pesan .= "Terima kasih atas kerja sama Ayah/Bunda dalam mendampingi muroja'ah Ananda di rumah.";

    return "https://wa.me/$hp?text=" . rawurlencode($pesan);
}

function buatLinkWaPR($no_hp, $nama_santri, $nama_ortu, $tanggal, $judul, $deskripsi, $nama_pengajar = 'Ustadz/Ustadzah') {
    $hp = preg_replace('/[^0-9]/', '', $no_hp ?? '');
    if (empty($hp)) return '';
    if (strpos($hp, '0') === 0) {
        $hp = '62' . substr($hp, 1);
    }

    $tgl_indo = date('d/m/Y', strtotime($tanggal));
    $ortu_sapaan = !empty($nama_ortu) ? "Bapak/Ibu *$nama_ortu*" : "Orang Tua/Wali";

    $pesan  = "Assalamu'alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuh,\n";
    $pesan .= "Yth. $ortu_sapaan,\n\n";
    $pesan .= "Terdapat *Tugas Rumah (PR)* untuk Ananda *$nama_santri* dari *Surau Tahfizh Firdaus*:\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Tanggal:* $tgl_indo\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Judul Tugas:* $judul\n";
    $pesan .= "• *Instruksi:* $deskripsi\n";
    $pesan .= "----------------------------------\n";
    $pesan .= "Mohon bantuan Ayah/Bunda untuk menyimak tugas Ananda di rumah dan menandainya selesai di Portal Orang Tua. Terima kasih.\n";
    $pesan .= "— *$nama_pengajar*";

    return "https://wa.me/$hp?text=" . rawurlencode($pesan);
}

// 9. Fungsi Render Tombol Switcher "Khusus Lokal Binaan" vs "Tampilkan Semua Santri (Badal)"
if (!function_exists('renderBadgeFilterBinaan')) {
    function renderBadgeFilterBinaan($role) {
        if (in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan'])) return '';

        $is_semua = (isset($_GET['semua']) && $_GET['semua'] === '1');
        $params   = $_GET;
        $params['semua'] = $is_semua ? '0' : '1';
        $url_toggle = basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF']) . '?' . http_build_query($params);

        if ($is_semua) {
            return '
            <a href="' . htmlspecialchars($url_toggle) . '" title="Kembali ke santri lokal binaan saya"
               class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-100 transition shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="users" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                <span>Mode Badal: Semua Santri</span>
                <span class="underline ml-1 font-bold">Reset ke Binaan</span>
            </a>';
        }
        return '
            <a href="' . htmlspecialchars($url_toggle) . '" title="Klik jika Anda sedang menggantikan ustadz/ah lain (Badal)"
               class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-100 transition shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                <span>Filter: Santri Binaan Saya</span>
                <span class="text-slate-400">|</span>
                <span class="underline text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900">Tampilkan Semua</span>
            </a>';
    }
}

===== includes\auth.php =====
<?php
/** Shared session helpers for SIRAH pages and API handlers. */
function startAppSession(): void
{
    if (session_status() !== PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
        $isHttps = isset($_SERVER['HTTPS']) && $_SERVER['HTTPS'] !== '' && strtolower($_SERVER['HTTPS']) !== 'off';
        session_set_cookie_params([
            'lifetime' => 0,
            'path' => '/',
            'secure' => $isHttps,
            'httponly' => true,
            'samesite' => 'Lax',
        ]);
        session_start();
    }

    verifyCsrfRequest();
}

function csrfToken(): string
{
    if (empty($_SESSION['csrf_token'])) {
        $_SESSION['csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
    }

    return $_SESSION['csrf_token'];
}

function csrfTokenField(): string
{
    return '<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="' . htmlspecialchars(csrfToken(), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') . '">';
}

function verifyCsrfRequest(): void
{
    if (($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? 'GET') !== 'POST') {
        return;
    }

    $submittedToken = $_POST['csrf_token'] ?? '';
    if (!is_string($submittedToken) || !hash_equals(csrfToken(), $submittedToken)) {
        http_response_code(403);
        exit('Permintaan ditolak. Muat ulang halaman lalu coba kembali.');
    }
}

function isAuthenticated(): bool
{
    return isset($_SESSION['user_id']);
}

function userHasRole(array $roles): bool
{
    return isset($_SESSION['role']) && in_array($_SESSION['role'], $roles, true);
}

===== pages\rapor\rekap_bulanan.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$bulan_dipilih = isset($_GET['bulan']) ? str_pad((int)$_GET['bulan'], 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT) : date('m');
$tahun_dipilih = isset($_GET['tahun']) ? (int)$_GET['tahun'] : (int)date('Y');
$id_lokal      = isset($_GET['id_lokal']) ? (int)$_GET['id_lokal'] : 0;
$periode       = $tahun_dipilih . '-' . $bulan_dipilih;

$nama_bulan = [
    '01' => 'Januari', '02' => 'Februari', '03' => 'Maret', '04' => 'April',
    '05' => 'Mei', '06' => 'Juni', '07' => 'Juli', '08' => 'Agustus',
    '09' => 'September', '10' => 'Oktober', '11' => 'November', '12' => 'Desember'
];

// Ambil daftar lokal untuk filter
$lokal_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah FROM halaqah ORDER BY id ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
$nama_lokal_terpilih = "Semua Kelas / Lokal";
foreach ($lokal_list as $lk) {
    if ((int)$lk['id'] === $id_lokal) {
        $nama_lokal_terpilih = $lk['nama_halaqah'];
        break;
    }
}

$where_lokal = $id_lokal > 0 ? "WHERE s.id_halaqah = $id_lokal" : "";

// Query akurat menggunakan Subquery terpisah untuk Hafalan (h) dan Absensi (a)
// agar TIDAK terjadi duplikasi perkalian baris (Cartesian Product)
$sql_santri = "SELECT 
                  s.id, s.nama, s.nis, hl.nama_halaqah,
                  COALESCE(h.total_ayat, 0) as total_ayat,
                  COALESCE(h.total_murajaah, 0) as total_murajaah,
                  COALESCE(h.total_tasmi, 0) as total_tasmi,
                  COALESCE(a.hadir, 0) as hadir,
                  COALESCE(a.sakit, 0) as sakit,
                  COALESCE(a.izin, 0) as izin,
                  COALESCE(a.alpa, 0) as alpa
               FROM santri s
               LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
               LEFT JOIN (
                   SELECT id_santri,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END) as total_ayat,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_murajaah,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_tasmi
                   FROM hafalan
                   WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                   GROUP BY id_santri
               ) h ON s.id = h.id_santri
               LEFT JOIN (
                   SELECT id_santri,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'H' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as hadir,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as sakit,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'I' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as izin,
                          SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(LEFT(TRIM(status), 1)) = 'A' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as alpa
                   FROM absensi
                   WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                   GROUP BY id_santri
               ) a ON s.id = a.id_santri
               $where_lokal
               ORDER BY s.nama ASC";

$data_santri = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $sql_santri), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Rekap Bulanan (" . $nama_bulan[$bulan_dipilih] . " " . $tahun_dipilih . ") - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">

                <div>

                </div>
            </div>
            <button onclick="window.print()" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold py-2.5 px-5 rounded-xl transition shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">

        <!-- Filter Periode & Lokal -->

                    <select name="id_lokal" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none">
                        <option value="0">Semua Kelas / Lokal</option>

===== pages\rapor\input.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Daftar 37 Surat Juz 30 sesuai urutan di Excel Surau
$surat_juz_30 = [
    "An- Naba'", "An- Nazi'at", "Abasa", "At- Takwir", "Al- Infithar",
    "Al- Muthaffifin", "Al- Insyiqaq", "Al- Buruj", "Ath- Thariq", "Al-A'la",
    "Al- Ghasyiyyah", "Al- Fajr", "Al- Balad", "Asy- Syams", "Al- Lail",
    "Ad- Dhuha", "Asy- Syarth", "At- Tiin", "Al-'Alaq", "Al-Qadr",
    "Al- Bayyinah", "Az- Zalzalah", "Al- 'Adiyat", "Al- Qari'ah", "At- Takatsur",
    "Al- 'Ashr", "Al- Humazah", "Al- Fiil", "Quraisy", "Al- Ma'un",
    "Al- Kautsar", "Al- Kafirun", "An- Nashr", "Al- Lahab", "Al- Ikhlash",
    "Al- Falaq", "An- Naas"
];

// Ambil daftar santri beserta Lokal/Kelas & Ustadz Pengampunya
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, s.jenis_kelamin, s.id_halaqah, 
                        h.nama_halaqah, h.keterangan as ket_lokal,
                        COALESCE(u.nama_lengkap, h.nama_pengajar) as pengajar_lokal
                 FROM santri s
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id
                 LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                 $where_binaan
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_res = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$lokal_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah, keterangan FROM halaqah ORDER BY id ASC");
$lokal_list = $lokal_res ? mysqli_fetch_all($lokal_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

$id_santri    = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$juz          = isset($_GET['juz']) ? (int) $_GET['juz'] : 30; // Default Juz 30
$semester     = isset($_GET['semester']) ? $_GET['semester'] : '1 (Satu)';
$tahun_ajaran = isset($_GET['tahun_ajaran']) ? $_GET['tahun_ajaran'] : date('Y');

$santri_aktif = null;
foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ((int)$s['id'] === $id_santri) {
        $santri_aktif = $s;
        break;
    }
}

// Ambil Data Rapor jika sudah pernah disimpan
$header_data = null;
$detail_data = [];
$absensi_db  = ['S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];

if ($santri_aktif) {
    $sem_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $semester);
    $thn_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tahun_ajaran);
    
    // Cek tabel rapor_tahfidz
    $cek_tbl = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW TABLES LIKE 'rapor_tahfidz'");
    if ($cek_tbl && mysqli_num_rows($cek_tbl) > 0) {
        $header_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz AND semester = '$sem_esc' AND tahun_ajaran = '$thn_esc' LIMIT 1");
        if ($header_q) {
            $header_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($header_q);
            if ($header_data) {
                $detail_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = {$header_data['id']} ORDER BY urutan ASC");
                $detail_data = mysqli_fetch_all($detail_q, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
            }
        }
    }

    // Hitung otomatis Sakit, Izin, Alfa dari tabel absensi sebagai nilai default
    $q_abs = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT status, COUNT(*) as jml FROM absensi WHERE id_santri = $id_santri GROUP BY status");
    if ($q_abs) {
        while ($ab = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_abs)) {
            $in = strtoupper(substr(trim($ab['status']), 0, 1));
            if (isset($absensi_db[$in])) $absensi_db[$in] += (int)$ab['jml'];
        }
    }
}

// Kelas Otomatis dari Lokal Halaqah Santri
if (!empty($santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'])) {
    $default_kelas = $santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'];
} elseif (!empty($header_data['kelas'])) {
    $default_kelas = $header_data['kelas'];
} else {
    $default_kelas = ($santri_aktif && $santri_aktif['jenis_kelamin'] === 'P') ? 'Khusus Perempuan' : 'Khusus Laki-Laki';
}

$default_judul = $header_data['judul_materi'] ?? "Materi Tahfidz (Juz $juz)";
$default_wali  = $header_data['wali_kelas'] ?? (($santri_aktif['pengajar_lokal'] ?? '') ?: 'Ust. Hadi Latif, M.Pd');

// Siapkan daftar baris materi default jika belum pernah disimpan di DB
$baris_form = [];
if (count($detail_data) > 0) {
    foreach ($detail_data as $d) {
        $baris_form[] = ['materi' => $d['nama_materi'], 'nilai' => $d['nilai']];
    }
} else {
    if ($juz === 30) {
        // Khusus Juz 30: Otomatis 37 Surat
        foreach ($surat_juz_30 as $nama_srt) {
            $baris_form[] = ['materi' => $nama_srt, 'nilai' => ''];
        }
    } else {
        // Selain Juz 30 (Juz 1 - 29): Otomatis 20 Pojok sesuai halaman Mushaf
        for ($i = 1; $i <= 20; $i++) {
            $hal = ($juz - 1) * 20 + $i + 1;
            $label_hal = ($juz === 1 && $i === 1) ? "Al-fatihah + Halaman 2" : "Halaman $hal";
            $baris_form[] = ['materi' => $label_hal, 'nilai' => ''];
        }
    }
}
?>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
