if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$role    = $_SESSION['role'];
$user_id = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Fungsi konversi angka ke kata Terbilang Bahasa Indonesia
function terbilang_indonesia($angka) {
    $angka = abs((int) round($angka));
    $baca  = ["", "Satu", "Dua", "Tiga", "Empat", "Lima", "Enam", "Tujuh", "Delapan", "Sembilan", "Sepuluh", "Sebelas"];
    if ($angka === 0) return "Nol";
    
    $terbilang = "";
    if ($angka < 12) {
        $terbilang = " " . $baca[$angka];
    } elseif ($angka < 20) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia($angka - 10) . " Belas";
    } elseif ($angka < 100) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 10)) . " Puluh" . ($angka % 10 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 10) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 200) {
        $terbilang = " Seratus" . ($angka - 100 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka - 100) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 1000) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 100)) . " Ratus" . ($angka % 100 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 100) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 2000) {
        $terbilang = " Seribu" . ($angka - 1000 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka - 1000) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 1000000) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 1000)) . " Ribu" . ($angka % 1000 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 1000) : "");
    }
    return trim($terbilang);
}

// Daftar 37 Surat Juz 30 untuk fallback tampilan jika belum diinput
$default_surat_juz30 = [
    "An- Naba'", "An- Nazi'at", "Abasa", "At- Takwir", "Al- Infithar",
    "Al- Muthaffifin", "Al- Insyiqaq", "Al- Buruj", "Ath- Thariq", "Al-A'la",
    "Al- Ghasyiyyah", "Al- Fajr", "Al- Balad", "Asy- Syams", "Al- Lail",
    "Ad- Dhuha", "Asy- Syarth", "At- Tiin", "Al-'Alaq", "Al-Qadr",
    "Al- Bayyinah", "Az- Zalzalah", "Al- 'Adiyat", "Al- Qari'ah", "At- Takatsur",
    "Al- 'Ashr", "Al- Humazah", "Al- Fiil", "Quraisy", "Al- Ma'un",
    "Al- Kautsar", "Al- Kafirun", "An- Nashr", "Al- Lahab", "Al- Ikhlash",
    "Al- Falaq", "An- Naas"
];

// Ambil daftar santri beserta Lokal/Kelas & Ustadz Pengampunya
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';
if ($role === 'ortu') {
    $where_filter = "WHERE s.id_user_ortu = $user_id";
} else {
    $where_filter = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $user_id, $role, 's');
}

$santri_query = "SELECT s.*, h.nama_halaqah, h.keterangan as ket_lokal,
                        COALESCE(u.nama_lengkap, h.nama_pengajar) as pengajar_lokal
                 FROM santri s
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id
                 LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                 $where_filter
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_res = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri    = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
// Dukungan parameter mode_juz ('30', '1-2', '2-3', atau '1', '2', dst.)
$mode_juz     = $_GET['mode_juz'] ?? ($_GET['juz'] ?? '30');
$semester     = isset($_GET['semester']) ? $_GET['semester'] : '1 (Satu)';
$tahun_ajaran = isset($_GET['tahun_ajaran']) ? $_GET['tahun_ajaran'] : date('Y');

$santri_aktif = null;
foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ((int)$s['id'] === $id_santri) {
        $santri_aktif = $s;
        break;
    }
}

// Parse daftar Juz yang akan ditampilkan (Bisa 1 Juz seperti [30] atau 2 Juz seperti [1, 2])
$juz_tampil_list = [];
if (strpos($mode_juz, '-') !== false) {
    $parts = explode('-', $mode_juz);
    $juz_tampil_list[] = (int) $parts[0];
    $juz_tampil_list[] = (int) $parts[1];
} else {
    $juz_tampil_list[] = (int) $mode_juz;
}

// Fungsi ambil data 1 Juz dari database (beserta fallback 37 surat atau 20 pojok)
function ambilDataRaporJuz($conn, $id_santri, $juz_num, $semester, $tahun_ajaran, $default_surat_juz30) {
    $sem_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $semester);
    $thn_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tahun_ajaran);
    
    $header = null;
    $details = [];
    $cek_tbl = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW TABLES LIKE 'rapor_tahfidz'");
    if ($cek_tbl && mysqli_num_rows($cek_tbl) > 0 && $id_santri > 0) {
        $q_h = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz_num AND semester = '$sem_esc' AND tahun_ajaran = '$thn_esc' LIMIT 1");
        if ($q_h) {
            $header = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_h);
            if ($header) {
                $q_d = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = {$header['id']} ORDER BY urutan ASC");
                $details = mysqli_fetch_all($q_d, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
            }
        }
    }

    // Jika belum ada detail di DB, bangun kerangka baris default (37 surat utk Juz 30, 20 pojok utk lainnya)
    if (empty($details)) {
        if ($juz_num === 30) {
            foreach ($default_surat_juz30 as $idx => $srt) {
                $details[] = ['urutan' => $idx + 1, 'nama_materi' => $srt, 'nilai' => null];
            }
        } else {
            for ($i = 1; $i <= 20; $i++) {
                $hal = ($juz_num - 1) * 20 + $i + 1;
                $materi = ($juz_num === 1 && $i === 1) ? "Al-fatihah + Halaman 2" : "Halaman $hal";
                $details[] = ['urutan' => $i, 'nama_materi' => $materi, 'nilai' => null];
            }
        }
    }

    return ['header' => $header, 'details' => $details];
}

$rapor_per_juz = [];
$header_utama  = null;
foreach ($juz_tampil_list as $jz) {
    $data_jz = ambilDataRaporJuz($conn, $santri_aktif['id'] ?? 0, $jz, $semester, $tahun_ajaran, $default_surat_juz30);
    $rapor_per_juz[$jz] = $data_jz;
    if (!$header_utama && $data_jz['header']) {
        $header_utama = $data_jz['header'];
    }
}

// Kelas Otomatis dari Lokal Halaqah Santri
if (!empty($santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'])) {
    $kelas_tampil = $santri_aktif['nama_halaqah'];
} elseif (!empty($header_utama['kelas'])) {
    $kelas_tampil = $header_utama['kelas'];
} else {
    $kelas_tampil = ($santri_aktif && $santri_aktif['jenis_kelamin'] === 'P') ? 'Khusus Perempuan' : 'Khusus Laki-Laki';
}

$wali_kelas_tampil = !empty($header_utama['wali_kelas']) ? $header_utama['wali_kelas'] : (!empty($santri_aktif['pengajar_lokal']) ? $santri_aktif['pengajar_lokal'] : 'Ust. Hadi Latif, M.Pd');
$kepala_stq_tampil = !empty($header_utama['kepala_stq']) ? $header_utama['kepala_stq'] : 'Ust. Aipda Syaiful Hendra';
$tempat_tampil     = !empty($header_utama['tempat_cetak']) ? $header_utama['tempat_cetak'] : 'Jalikur Patanangan';

                $pembagi_default = ($jz_num === 30) ? 37 : 20;

            <table class="tabel-rapor">

                    <?php 
                    $total_nilai = 0;
                    $jml_terisi  = 0;

                    foreach ($d_jz as $idx => $baris):
                        $raw_val = $baris['nilai'];
                        $has_val = ($raw_val !== null && $raw_val !== '');
                        $angka   = $has_val ? (int) $raw_val : null;

                        if ($has_val) {
                            $total_nilai += $angka;
                            $jml_terisi++;
                            // Rumus Excel Surau: >=90 = A (Baik), >=85 = B (Diterima), <=84 = C (Gagal)
                            if ($angka >= 90) {
                                $prediket = 'A'; $ket = 'Baik';
                            } elseif ($angka >= 85) {
                                $prediket = 'B'; $ket = 'Diterima';
                            } else {
                                $prediket = 'C'; $ket = 'Gagal';
                            }
                        } else {
                            $prediket = ''; $ket = '';
                        }
                    ?>
                    <tr>
                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $baris['urutan'] ?? ($idx + 1) ?></td>
                        <td><?= htmlspecialchars($baris['nama_materi']) ?></td>
                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $has_val ? $angka : '' ?></td>
                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $prediket ?></td>
                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $ket ?></td>
                    </tr>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>

                    <?php 
                    $pembagi = $jml_terisi > 0 ? $jml_terisi : $pembagi_default;
                    $rata_rata = $total_nilai > 0 ? ($total_nilai / $pembagi) : 0;
                    $terbilang_total = $total_nilai > 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($total_nilai) : '-';
                    $terbilang_rata  = $rata_rata > 0 ? terbilang_indonesia(round($rata_rata)) : '-';
                    ?>
                    <tr style="font-weight: bold;">
                        <td colspan="2">Total Nilai</td>
                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $total_nilai > 0 ? $total_nilai : '' ?></td>
                        <td colspan="2" style="text-align: center; font-style: italic; font-size: 11px;"><?= $terbilang_total ?></td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr style="font-weight: bold;">
                        <td colspan="2"><?= $jz_num === 30 ? 'Rata-Rata Nilai' : 'Rata-Rata Nilai Tahfidz' ?></td>
                        <td style="text-align: center;"><?= $rata_rata > 0 ? round($rata_rata, 2) : '' ?></td>
                        <td colspan="2" style="text-align: center; font-style: italic; font-size: 11px;"><?= $terbilang_rata ?></td>
                    </tr>
                </tbody>
            </table>
            <?php endforeach; ?>

            <!-- BAGIAN KHUSUS RAPOR JUZ 30: TABEL UJIAN JUZ, TAHSIN, KEPRIBADIAN, ABSENSI & CATATAN -->

                $arr_ujian = array_filter([$u30, $u1, $u2, $u3, $u4], fn($v) => $v !== null && $v !== '');
                $jml_ujian = array_sum($arr_ujian);
                $rata_ujian = count($arr_ujian) > 0 ? round($jml_ujian / 5, 1) : 0;
            ?>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px; margin-top: 8px; margin-bottom: 12px;">
                <!-- Tabel Ujian Lintas Juz -->

                            <tr><td>Juz 4</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $u4 ?? '-' ?></td></tr>
                            <tr style="font-weight: bold;"><td>JUMLAH</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $jml_ujian > 0 ? $jml_ujian : '-' ?></td></tr>
                            <tr style="font-weight: bold;"><td>RATA-RATA</td><td style="text-align: center;"><?= $rata_ujian > 0 ? $rata_ujian : '-' ?></td></tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <!-- Nilai Tahsin, Naik ke Juz & Remedi -->

===== pages\rapor\syahadah.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'], true)) {
    header('Location: login.php');
    exit;
}

$id_santri = filter_input(INPUT_GET, 'id_santri', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT) ?: 0;
$id_tasmi = filter_input(INPUT_GET, 'id_tasmi', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT) ?: 0;
$role = $_SESSION['role'];
$id_user = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_user, $role, 's');
$and_binaan = $where_binaan !== '' ? ' AND ' . preg_replace('/^\s*WHERE\s+/i', '', $where_binaan) : '';

$query = "SELECT s.nama, s.nama_ortu, s.nis, h.tanggal, h.juz, h.pojok, h.surat, h.predikat, h.catatan, u.nama_lengkap AS nama_pengajar
          FROM hafalan h
          JOIN santri s ON s.id = h.id_santri
          LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = h.id_ustadz
          WHERE h.id = ? AND h.id_santri = ? AND h.jenis = 'tasmi' $and_binaan
          LIMIT 1";
$stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, $query);
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'ii', $id_tasmi, $id_santri);
mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
$result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
$syahadah = $result ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($result) : null;
mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

if (!$syahadah || !in_array($syahadah['predikat'], ['A', 'B'], true)) {
    http_response_code(404);
    exit('Data syahadah tidak ditemukan.');
}

$page_title = "Syahadah Tasmi' - " . htmlspecialchars($syahadah['nama'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>
<body class="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 p-6 text-slate-800">
    <main class="syahadah mx-auto max-w-5xl rounded-3xl border-8 border-double border-amber-500 bg-white p-10 text-center shadow-xl md:p-16">
        <p class="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.35em] text-amber-700">Surau Tahfizh Firdaus</p>
        <h1 class="mt-5 text-4xl font-extrabold tracking-wide text-slate-900">SYAHADAH TASMI'</h1>
        <p class="mt-3 text-slate-500">Diberikan dengan penuh penghargaan kepada</p>
        <h2 class="mt-6 text-4xl font-bold text-indigo-900"><?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['nama'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></h2>
        <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['nis'] ?? '-', ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></p>
        <p class="mx-auto mt-8 max-w-3xl leading-8 text-slate-700">
            Atas keberhasilan menyelesaikan ujian tasmi' hafalan Al-Qur'an
            <strong><?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['surat'] ?: ('Juz ' . ($syahadah['juz'] ?? '-')), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></strong>
            dengan predikat <strong><?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['predikat'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></strong>.
        </p>
        <?php if (!empty($syahadah['pojok'])): ?>
            <p class="mt-2 text-slate-600">Capaian: <?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['pojok'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?> pojok</p>
        <?php endif; ?>
        <p class="mt-8 text-sm text-slate-500">Semoga Allah memberkahi hafalan dan mengamalkannya.</p>
        <div class="mt-14 flex justify-between gap-10 text-sm text-slate-600">
            <div class="flex-1"><p><?= date('d F Y', strtotime($syahadah['tanggal'])) ?></p><p class="mt-16 border-t border-slate-400 pt-2"><?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['nama_pengajar'] ?? 'Penguji', ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></p><p>Penguji</p></div>
            <div class="flex-1"><p>Surau Tahfizh Firdaus</p><p class="mt-16 border-t border-slate-400 pt-2"><?= htmlspecialchars($syahadah['nama_ortu'] ?? $syahadah['nama'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') ?></p><p>Wali Santri</p></div>
        </div>
    </main>
    <div class="no-print mx-auto mt-5 flex max-w-5xl justify-center gap-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white">Cetak Syahadah</button>
        <a href="input_tasmi.php" class="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">Kembali</a>
    </div>
<?php include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/footer.php'; ?>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Pastikan kolom juz & pojok ada agar query tabel riwayat selalu aman
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// Hapus riwayat setoran jika diminta
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, (int) $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');
$and_binaan = $where_binaan !== '' ? ' AND ' . preg_replace('/^\s*WHERE\s+/i', '', $where_binaan) : '';
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_POST['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE h FROM hafalan h JOIN santri s ON s.id = h.id_santri WHERE h.id = $id_hapus AND h.jenis = 'ziyadah' $and_binaan");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data setoran ziyadah berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_ziyadah.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil daftar santri khusus lokal binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang login
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_result = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil riwayat ziyadah hari ini (khusus santri binaan)
$jenis_halaman = 'ziyadah'; 
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM hafalan h 
                  JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE h.tanggal = CURDATE() AND h.jenis = '$jenis_halaman' $and_binaan
                  ORDER BY h.id DESC";
$riwayat_result = mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query);
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all($riwayat_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung ringkasan hari ini
$total_setoran_hari_ini = count($riwayat_list);
$total_ayat_hari_ini = 0;
foreach ($riwayat_list as $r) {
    $total_ayat_hari_ini += (int)($r['jumlah_ayat'] ?? 0);
}

// Daftar 114 Surat untuk Autocomplete
$daftar_surat = ["Al-Fatihah","Al-Baqarah","Ali 'Imran","An-Nisa'","Al-Ma'idah","Al-An'am","Al-A'raf","Al-Anfal","At-Taubah","Yunus","Hud","Yusuf","Ar-Ra'd","Ibrahim","Al-Hijr","An-Nahl","Al-Isra'","Al-Kahf","Maryam","Taha","Al-Anbiya'","Al-Hajj","Al-Mu'minun","An-Nur","Al-Furqan","Asy-Syu'ara'","An-Naml","Al-Qasas","Al-'Ankabut","Ar-Rum","Luqman","As-Sajdah","Al-Ahzab","Saba'","Fatir","Yasin","As-Saffat","Sad","Az-Zumar","Gafir","Fussilat","Asy-Syura","Az-Zukhruf","Ad-Dukhan","Al-Jasiyah","Al-Ahqaf","Muhammad","Al-Fath","Al-Hujurat","Qaf","Az-Zariyat","At-Tur","An-Najm","Al-Qamar","Ar-Rahman","Al-Waqi'ah","Al-Hadid","Al-Mujadilah","Al-Hasyr","Al-Mumtahanah","As-Saff","Al-Jumu'ah","Al-Munafiqun","At-Tagabun","At-Talaq","At-Tahrim","Al-Mulk","Al-Qalam","Al-Haqqah","Al-Ma'arij","Nuh","Al-Jinn","Al-Muzzammil","Al-Muddassir","Al-Qiyamah","Al-Insan","Al-Mursalat","An-Naba'","An-Nazi'at","'Abasa","At-Takwir","Al-Infitar","Al-Mutaffifin","Al-Insyiqaq","Al-Buruj","At-Tariq","Al-A'la","Al-Gasyiyah","Al-Fajr","Al-Balad","Asy-Syams","Al-Lail","Ad-Duha","Asy-Syarh","At-Tin","Al-'Alaq","Al-Qadr","Al-Bayyinah","Az-Zalzalah","Al-'Adiyat","Al-Qari'ah","At-Takasur","Al-'Asr","Al-Humazah","Al-Fil","Quraisy","Al-Ma'un","Al-Kausar","Al-Kafirun","An-Nasr","Al-Lahab","Al-Ikhlas","Al-Falaq","An-Nas"];
?>

===== pages\hafalan\tasmi.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_ustadz, $role, 's');
$and_binaan = $where_binaan !== '' ? ' AND ' . preg_replace('/^\s*WHERE\s+/i', '', $where_binaan) : '';

// Pastikan kolom juz & pojok ada di tabel hafalan
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// Hapus riwayat tasmi jika diminta
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_POST['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE h FROM hafalan h JOIN santri s ON s.id = h.id_santri WHERE h.id = $id_hapus AND h.jenis = 'tasmi' $and_binaan");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data ujian tasmi' berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_tasmi.php");
    exit;
}

// Filter santri khusus lokal binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Riwayat ujian tasmi (menampilkan 25 ujian terakhir dari santri binaan)
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM hafalan h 
                  JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE h.jenis = 'tasmi' $and_binaan 
                  ORDER BY h.tanggal DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 25";
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>

                                            </a>
                                        <?php endif; ?>

                                            <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>

===== pages\hafalan\murajaah.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Pastikan kolom juz & pojok ada di tabel hafalan
$cek_juz = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM hafalan LIKE 'juz'");
if ($cek_juz && mysqli_num_rows($cek_juz) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `juz` INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `jenis`");
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE hafalan ADD COLUMN `pojok` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL AFTER `juz`");
}

// Hapus riwayat setoran murajaah jika diminta
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, (int) $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');
$and_binaan = $where_binaan !== '' ? ' AND ' . preg_replace('/^\s*WHERE\s+/i', '', $where_binaan) : '';
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_POST['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE h FROM hafalan h JOIN santri s ON s.id = h.id_santri WHERE h.id = $id_hapus AND h.jenis = 'murajaah' $and_binaan");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data setoran muraja'ah berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: input_murajaah.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil daftar santri khusus lokal binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang login
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_result = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil riwayat murajaah hari ini (khusus santri binaan)
$jenis_halaman = 'murajaah'; 
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM hafalan h 
                  JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE h.tanggal = CURDATE() AND h.jenis = '$jenis_halaman' $and_binaan
                  ORDER BY h.id DESC";
$riwayat_result = mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query);
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all($riwayat_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung ringkasan hari ini
$total_setoran_hari_ini = count($riwayat_list);
$total_ayat_hari_ini = 0;
foreach ($riwayat_list as $r) {
    $total_ayat_hari_ini += (int)($r['jumlah_ayat'] ?? 0);
}

// Daftar 114 Surat untuk Autocomplete
$daftar_surat = ["Al-Fatihah","Al-Baqarah","Ali 'Imran","An-Nisa'","Al-Ma'idah","Al-An'am","Al-A'raf","Al-Anfal","At-Taubah","Yunus","Hud","Yusuf","Ar-Ra'd","Ibrahim","Al-Hijr","An-Nahl","Al-Isra'","Al-Kahf","Maryam","Taha","Al-Anbiya'","Al-Hajj","Al-Mu'minun","An-Nur","Al-Furqan","Asy-Syu'ara'","An-Naml","Al-Qasas","Al-'Ankabut","Ar-Rum","Luqman","As-Sajdah","Al-Ahzab","Saba'","Fatir","Yasin","As-Saffat","Sad","Az-Zumar","Gafir","Fussilat","Asy-Syura","Az-Zukhruf","Ad-Dukhan","Al-Jasiyah","Al-Ahqaf","Muhammad","Al-Fath","Al-Hujurat","Qaf","Az-Zariyat","At-Tur","An-Najm","Al-Qamar","Ar-Rahman","Al-Waqi'ah","Al-Hadid","Al-Mujadilah","Al-Hasyr","Al-Mumtahanah","As-Saff","Al-Jumu'ah","Al-Munafiqun","At-Tagabun","At-Talaq","At-Tahrim","Al-Mulk","Al-Qalam","Al-Haqqah","Al-Ma'arij","Nuh","Al-Jinn","Al-Muzzammil","Al-Muddassir","Al-Qiyamah","Al-Insan","Al-Mursalat","An-Naba'","An-Nazi'at","'Abasa","At-Takwir","Al-Infitar","Al-Mutaffifin","Al-Insyiqaq","Al-Buruj","At-Tariq","Al-A'la","Al-Gasyiyah","Al-Fajr","Al-Balad","Asy-Syams","Al-Lail","Ad-Duha","Asy-Syarh","At-Tin","Al-'Alaq","Al-Qadr","Al-Bayyinah","Az-Zalzalah","Al-'Adiyat","Al-Qari'ah","At-Takasur","Al-'Asr","Al-Humazah","Al-Fil","Quraisy","Al-Ma'un","Al-Kausar","Al-Kafirun","An-Nasr","Al-Lahab","Al-Ikhlas","Al-Falaq","An-Nas"];
?>

===== pages\hafalan\kehadiran.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Operator, Ustadz/ah, dan Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$nama_user = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? '';
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// Pastikan kolom status di tabel absensi mendukung format lengkap ('Hadir','Sakit','Izin','Alfa')
// sekaligus konversi data lama ('H','S','I','A') jika pernah tersimpan
mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE absensi MODIFY COLUMN `status` VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Hadir'");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Hadir' WHERE status = 'H' OR status = ''");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Sakit' WHERE status = 'S'");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Izin'  WHERE status = 'I'");
mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = 'Alfa'  WHERE status = 'A'");

// Proses simpan absensi
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['status'])) {
    $tanggal  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal']);
    $id_lokal = isset($_POST['id_lokal']) ? (int) $_POST['id_lokal'] : 0;

    $map_lengkap = [
        'H' => 'Hadir', 'Hadir' => 'Hadir',
        'S' => 'Sakit', 'Sakit' => 'Sakit',
        'I' => 'Izin',  'Izin'  => 'Izin',
        'A' => 'Alfa',  'Alfa'  => 'Alfa'
    ];

    foreach ($_POST['status'] as $id_santri => $st_raw) {
        $id_s       = (int) $id_santri;
        if (!santriDalamBinaan($conn, $id_s, $id_ustadz, $role)) {
            continue;
        }
        $status_fix = $map_lengkap[$st_raw] ?? 'Hadir';
        $keterangan = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan'][$id_s] ?? ''));
        
        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM absensi WHERE id_santri = $id_s AND tanggal = '$tanggal'");
        if ($cek && mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = '$status_fix', keterangan = '$keterangan', id_ustadz = $id_ustadz WHERE id_santri = $id_s AND tanggal = '$tanggal'");
        } else {
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO absensi (id_santri, tanggal, status, keterangan, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_s, '$tanggal', '$status_fix', '$keterangan', $id_ustadz)");
        }
    }
    
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data kehadiran tanggal " . date('d F Y', strtotime($tanggal)) . " berhasil disimpan!";
    $q_lokal = $id_lokal > 0 ? "&id_lokal=$id_lokal" : "";
    header("Location: input_kehadiran.php?tanggal=$tanggal" . $q_lokal);
    exit;
}

// Ambil tanggal (default hari ini)
$tanggal  = $_GET['tanggal'] ?? date('Y-m-d');
$id_lokal = isset($_GET['id_lokal']) ? (int) $_GET['id_lokal'] : 0;

// Ambil daftar lokal (Jika Ustadz, utamakan lokal binaannya)
$where_lokal_opsi = "";
if (!$is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $u_info = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
    $id_h_user = (int) ($u_info['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nm_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_info['nama_lengkap'] ?? $nama_user));
    $where_lokal_opsi = "WHERE (id_ustadz = $id_ustadz OR id = $id_h_user OR LOWER(nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nm_esc'))";
}
$lokal_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_halaqah FROM halaqah $where_lokal_opsi ORDER BY id ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Jika Ustadz hanya mengampu 1 lokal dan belum memilih filter, otomatis pilih lokalnya
if ($id_lokal === 0 && !$is_pengelola_penuh && count($lokal_list) === 1) {
    $id_lokal = (int) $lokal_list[0]['id'];
}

// Ambil daftar santri sesuai filter lokal
$where_santri = $id_lokal > 0 ? "WHERE s.id_halaqah = $id_lokal" : "";
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_santri 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil data absensi yang sudah diinput untuk tanggal tersebut
$absensi_hari_ini = [];
$statistik = ['H' => 0, 'S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];

if (!empty($santri_list)) {
    $tgl_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tanggal);
    $absensi_result = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM absensi WHERE tanggal = '$tgl_esc'");
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($absensi_result)) {
        $absensi_hari_ini[$row['id_santri']] = $row;
    }

    foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
        if (isset($absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']])) {
            $in = strtoupper(substr(trim($absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']]['status']), 0, 1));
            if (isset($statistik[$in])) $statistik[$in]++;
        }
    }
}
?>

$page_title = "Input Kehadiran - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Operator, Ustadz/ah, atau Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom status_ortu di tabel pr jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_pr = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM pr LIKE 'status_ortu'");
if ($cek_kolom_pr && mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_pr) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE pr ADD COLUMN status_ortu ENUM('Belum','Selesai') DEFAULT 'Belum' AFTER deskripsi");
}

// Hapus PR jika diminta
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_POST['hapus'];
    $filter_pemilik = $is_pengelola_penuh ? '' : " AND id_ustadz = $id_ustadz";
    $deleted = mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM pr WHERE id = $id_hapus $filter_pemilik");
    $_SESSION['success'] = $deleted && mysqli_affected_rows($conn) > 0
        ? "Data Tugas Rumah (PR) berhasil dihapus."
        : "Data PR tidak ditemukan atau berada di luar kewenangan Anda.";
    header("Location: input_pr.php");
    exit;
}

// Filter santri khusus binaan Ustadz/Ustadzah (Operator & Pimpinan melihat semua)
$where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, $id_ustadz, $role, 's');
$santri_query = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM santri s 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id 
                 $where_binaan 
                 ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Proses Simpan PR Baru
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['judul'])) {   
    $tanggal   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal'] ?? date('Y-m-d'));
    $judul     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['judul']));
    $deskripsi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['deskripsi']));
    
    if ($_POST['id_santri'] === 'semua_binaan') {
        $jml_kirim = 0;
        foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
            $id_s = (int) $s['id'];
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO pr (id_santri, tanggal, judul, deskripsi, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_s, '$tanggal', '$judul', '$deskripsi', $id_ustadz)");
            $jml_kirim++;
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Tugas Rumah (PR) berhasil dikirimkan secara massal kepada $jml_kirim santri binaan!";
    } else {
        $id_santri = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO pr (id_santri, tanggal, judul, deskripsi, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal', '$judul', '$deskripsi', $id_ustadz)");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Tugas Rumah (PR) berhasil ditambahkan untuk santri terpilih!";
    }
    
    header("Location: input_pr.php"); 
    exit;
}

// Ambil riwayat PR yang diberikan (khusus santri binaan)
$and_binaan = !empty($where_binaan) ? str_replace('WHERE', 'AND', $where_binaan) : '';
$riwayat_query = "SELECT p.*, s.nama as nama_santri, s.nama_ortu, s.no_hp_ortu, hl.nama_halaqah 
                  FROM pr p 
                  JOIN santri s ON p.id_santri = s.id 
                  LEFT JOIN halaqah hl ON s.id_halaqah = hl.id
                  WHERE 1=1 $and_binaan 
                  ORDER BY p.tanggal DESC, p.id DESC LIMIT 30";
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung ringkasan status PR
$total_pr_tampil = count($riwayat_list);
$pr_sudah_disimak = 0;
foreach ($riwayat_list as $r) {
    if (($r['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum') === 'Selesai') $pr_sudah_disimak++;
}
?>

include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>

===== pages\halaqah\index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Operator, Pimpinan, dan Ustadz/Ustadzah yang dapat mengakses
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$role      = $_SESSION['role'];
$id_user   = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$nama_user = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? '';

// Apakah pengguna adalah Pengelola Penuh (Operator / Pimpinan)?
$is_pengelola_penuh = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']);

// Pastikan kolom `keterangan` dan `nama_pengajar` tersedia di tabel `halaqah`
$cek_ket = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM halaqah LIKE 'keterangan'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_ket) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE halaqah ADD COLUMN keterangan VARCHAR(150) DEFAULT 'Tahfidz Sore ( 16.30 - Isya )' AFTER tingkat");
}
$cek_pengajar = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM halaqah LIKE 'nama_pengajar'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_pengajar) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE halaqah ADD COLUMN nama_pengajar VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL AFTER id_ustadz");
}

// === 1. FITUR 1-KLIK: MUAT 31 LOKAL DEFAULT (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['seed_default_lokal']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $preset_lokal = [
        ['Karantina 1 Tahun 30 Juz (Ikhwan)', 'Muhammad Zaid Al Harits', 'Asrama'],

        $ket_prog = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $item[2]);

// === 2. TAMBAH KELAS / LOKAL BARU (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_kelas']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $nama_halaqah  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_halaqah']));
    $id_ustadz     = !empty($_POST['id_ustadz']) ? (int) $_POST['id_ustadz'] : 'NULL';
    $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_pengajar'] ?? ''));
    $keterangan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan']));

    if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL' && empty($nama_pengajar)) {
        $u_res = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
        $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $u_res['nama_lengkap'] ?? '');
    }

    $sql = "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah, id_ustadz, nama_pengajar, keterangan) 
            VALUES ('$nama_halaqah', $id_ustadz, '$nama_pengajar', '$keterangan')";
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        $new_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL') {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET id_halaqah = $new_id WHERE id = $id_ustadz");
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Lokal '$nama_halaqah' berhasil ditambahkan.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal menambahkan lokal: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. EDIT KELAS / LOKAL (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['edit_kelas']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $id_kelas      = (int) $_POST['id_kelas'];
    $nama_halaqah  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_halaqah']));
    $id_ustadz     = !empty($_POST['id_ustadz']) ? (int) $_POST['id_ustadz'] : 'NULL';
    $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_pengajar'] ?? ''));
    $keterangan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan']));

    if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL' && empty($nama_pengajar)) {
        $u_res = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_ustadz"));
        $nama_pengajar = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $u_res['nama_lengkap'] ?? '');
    }

    $sql = "UPDATE halaqah SET 
                nama_halaqah = '$nama_halaqah', 
                id_ustadz = $id_ustadz, 
                nama_pengajar = '$nama_pengajar', 
                keterangan = '$keterangan' 
            WHERE id = $id_kelas";
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        if ($id_ustadz !== 'NULL') {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET id_halaqah = $id_kelas WHERE id = $id_ustadz");
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data Lokal '$nama_halaqah' berhasil diperbarui.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php?id_kelas=$id_kelas");
    exit;
}

// === 4. HAPUS KELAS / LOKAL (KHUSUS OPERATOR / PIMPINAN) ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['hapus_kelas']) && $is_pengelola_penuh) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_POST['hapus_kelas'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET id_halaqah = NULL WHERE id_halaqah = $id_hapus");
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM halaqah WHERE id = $id_hapus");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data lokal berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php");
    exit;
}

// === 5. MASUKKAN SANTRI KE KELAS INI ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['assign_santri'])) {
    $id_kelas = (int) $_POST['id_kelas'];
    if (!empty($_POST['id_santri_arr']) && is_array($_POST['id_santri_arr'])) {
        $ids = array_map('intval', $_POST['id_santri_arr']);
        $id_list = implode(',', $ids);
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET id_halaqah = $id_kelas WHERE id IN ($id_list)");
        $_SESSION['success'] = count($ids) . " santri berhasil ditempatkan di lokal ini.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php?id_kelas=$id_kelas");
    exit;
}

// === 6. KELUARKAN SANTRI DARI KELAS ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['keluarkan_santri']) && isset($_POST['id_kelas'])) {
    $id_s = (int) $_POST['keluarkan_santri'];
    $id_k = (int) $_POST['id_kelas'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE santri SET id_halaqah = NULL WHERE id = $id_s");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Santri berhasil dikeluarkan dari lokal.";
    header("Location: kelola_kelas.php?id_kelas=$id_k");
    exit;
}

// =========================================================================
// FILTER DAFTAR KELAS BERDASARKAN AKTOR (OPERATOR/PIMPINAN vs USTADZ/AH)
// =========================================================================
$where_lokal = "";
if (!$is_pengelola_penuh) {
    // Ambil id_halaqah yang tercatat di tabel users untuk ustadz/ustadzah yang sedang login
    $u_login = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id_halaqah, nama_lengkap FROM users WHERE id = $id_user"));
    $id_halaqah_user = (int) ($u_login['id_halaqah'] ?? 0);
    $nama_ust_esc    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($u_login['nama_lengkap'] ?? $nama_user));

    // Tampilkan hanya lokal yang id_ustadz = user ini, ATAU id = id_halaqah di akun user, ATAU nama_pengajar sama dengan nama ustadz/ah
    $where_lokal = "WHERE (
                        h.id_ustadz = $id_user 
                        OR h.id = $id_halaqah_user 
                        OR (h.nama_pengajar IS NOT NULL AND h.nama_pengajar != '' AND LOWER(h.nama_pengajar) = LOWER('$nama_ust_esc'))
                    )";
}

$q_kelas = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_user_ustadz, u.username as user_ustadz,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM santri s WHERE s.id_halaqah = h.id) as jumlah_santri
            FROM halaqah h
            LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
            $where_lokal
            ORDER BY h.id ASC";
$kelas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $q_kelas), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung jumlah per kategori program untuk badge tab filter
$count_kat = ['all' => count($kelas_list), 'asrama' => 0, 'menginap' => 0, 'isya' => 0, 'maghrib' => 0];
$total_santri_terplot = 0;

foreach ($kelas_list as $kl) {
    $ket = $kl['keterangan'] ?? '';
    $total_santri_terplot += (int)$kl['jumlah_santri'];
    if (stripos($ket, 'Asrama') !== false) $count_kat['asrama']++;
    elseif (stripos($ket, 'Menginap') !== false) $count_kat['menginap']++;
    elseif (stripos($ket, 'Sebelum Maghrib') !== false) $count_kat['maghrib']++;
    else $count_kat['isya']++;
}

// Ambil semua Ustadz/Ustadzah untuk dropdown pilihan pengajar (khusus Operator/Pimpinan)
$ustadz_list = $is_pengelola_penuh 
    ? mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_lengkap, username FROM users WHERE role = 'ustadz' ORDER BY nama_lengkap ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC) 
    : [];

// Ambil semua santri untuk modal penempatan santri
$semua_santri = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT s.*, h.nama_halaqah FROM santri s LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON s.id_halaqah = h.id ORDER BY s.nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Tentukan Kelas yang sedang dipilih untuk dilihat detail santrinya
// Jika Ustadz/Ustadzah hanya mengampu 1 lokal, otomatis buka detail lokal tersebut!
$id_kelas_aktif = isset($_GET['id_kelas']) ? (int) $_GET['id_kelas'] : 0;
if ($id_kelas_aktif === 0 && !$is_pengelola_penuh && count($kelas_list) === 1) {
    $id_kelas_aktif = (int) $kelas_list[0]['id'];
}

$kelas_aktif = null;
$santri_di_kelas = [];

if ($id_kelas_aktif > 0) {
    foreach ($kelas_list as $kl) {
        if ((int)$kl['id'] === $id_kelas_aktif) {
            $kelas_aktif = $kl;
            break;
        }
    }
    if ($kelas_aktif) {
        $santri_di_kelas = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_halaqah = $id_kelas_aktif ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
}

function styleProgramLokal($ket) {
    if (stripos($ket, 'Asrama') !== false) {
        return ['dot' => 'bg-amber-500', 'badge' => 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200/80', 'icon' => 'building-2'];
    } elseif (stripos($ket, 'Menginap') !== false) {
        return ['dot' => 'bg-blue-500', 'badge' => 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200/80', 'icon' => 'moon'];
    } elseif (stripos($ket, 'Sebelum Maghrib') !== false) {
        return ['dot' => 'bg-emerald-500', 'badge' => 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200/80', 'icon' => 'sun'];
    }
    return ['dot' => 'bg-indigo-500', 'badge' => 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200/80', 'icon' => 'clock'];
}

                    <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Keterangan Program / Waktu</label>
                    <input type="text" name="keterangan" id="edit_keterangan" list="listKeterangan" required class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none">
                </div>
                <div class="pt-3 flex justify-end gap-2">
                    <button type="button" onclick="tutupModalEditKelas()" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Batal</button>
                    <button type="submit" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold py-2 px-5 rounded-xl text-xs shadow-sm">Simpan Perubahan</button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>
    <?php endif; ?>

    <!-- MODAL TEMPATKAN SANTRI KE LOKAL INI -->
    <?php if ($kelas_aktif): ?>
    <div id="modalAssignSantri" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 hidden bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden">
            <div class="px-6 py-4 bg-slate-900 text-white flex justify-between items-center">
                <div>
                    <h3 class="text-sm font-bold">Penempatan Santri: <?= htmlspecialchars($kelas_aktif['nama_halaqah']) ?></h3>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Centang santri yang bergabung di dalam lokal ini</p>

            </div>
            <form method="POST" class="p-6"><?php echo csrfTokenField(); ?>
                <input type="hidden" name="assign_santri" value="1">
                <input type="hidden" name="id_kelas" value="<?= $kelas_aktif['id'] ?>">

                <div class="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-xl divide-y divide-slate-100 mb-5">
                    <?php foreach ($semua_santri as $st): 
                        $sudah_di_sini = ((int)$st['id_halaqah'] === (int)$kelas_aktif['id']);

                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <input type="checkbox" name="id_santri_arr[]" value="<?= $st['id'] ?>" <?= $sudah_di_sini ? 'checked' : '' ?>
                                class="w-4 h-4 rounded text-indigo-600 focus:ring-indigo-500">

    <?php endif; ?>

===== api\santri\update.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri     = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    if (!santriDalamBinaan($conn, $id_santri, (int) $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'])) {
        http_response_code(403);
        exit('Akses santri ditolak.');
    }
    $id_user_ortu  = (int) $_POST['id_user_ortu'];
    $nama_santri   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_santri']));
    $nis           = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nis']));
    $jk            = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $tgl_lahir     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_lahir']);
    $alamat        = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['alamat']));
    $nama_ortu     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_ortu']));
    $no_hp_ortu    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['no_hp_ortu']));
    $username_ortu = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username_ortu']));
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');
    if ($password_baru !== '' && strlen($password_baru) < 12) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = 'Password baru minimal 12 karakter.';
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }
    $id_halaqah    = !empty($_POST['id_halaqah']) ? (int) $_POST['id_halaqah'] : "NULL";

    // 1. Cek duplikasi NIS pada santri lain
    $cek_nis = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM santri WHERE nis = '$nis' AND id != $id_santri");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_nis) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "NIS '$nis' sudah terdaftar pada santri lain!";
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }

    // 2. Update atau buat akun user orang tua
    if ($id_user_ortu > 0) {
        $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_ortu' AND id != $id_user_ortu");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username_ortu' sudah dipakai akun lain!";
            header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
            exit;
        }

        if (!empty($password_baru)) {
            $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_ortu', nama_lengkap = '$nama_ortu', password = '$hash' WHERE id = $id_user_ortu");
        } else {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_ortu', nama_lengkap = '$nama_ortu' WHERE id = $id_user_ortu");
        }
    } elseif (!empty($username_ortu)) {
        // Akun baru hanya dibuat dengan password yang ditentukan operator.
        if ($password_baru === '') {
            $_SESSION['error'] = 'Isi password minimal 12 karakter untuk membuat akun orang tua.';
            header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
            exit;
        }
        $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        if (mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('$username_ortu', '$hash', '$nama_ortu', 'ortu')")) {
            $id_user_ortu = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        }
    }

    // 3. Update tabel santri
    $id_ortu_sql = $id_user_ortu > 0 ? $id_user_ortu : "NULL";
    $sql_update = "UPDATE santri SET 
                    nama = '$nama_santri',
                    nis = '$nis',
                    jenis_kelamin = '$jk',
                    tanggal_lahir = '$tgl_lahir',
                    alamat = '$alamat',
                    nama_ortu = '$nama_ortu',
                    no_hp_ortu = '$no_hp_ortu',
                    id_user_ortu = $id_ortu_sql,
                    id_halaqah = $id_halaqah
                   WHERE id = $id_santri";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_update)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data santri '$nama_santri' berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal memperbarui data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api\santri\target.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya Ustadz dan Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    if (!santriDalamBinaan($conn, (int) ($_POST['id_santri'] ?? 0), (int) $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'])) {
        http_response_code(403);
        exit('Akses santri ditolak.');
    }
    // 1. Ambil dan bersihkan data
    $id_santri      = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $target_ayat    = (int) $_POST['target_ayat'];
    $tanggal_mulai  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_mulai']);
    $keterangan     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['keterangan']);

    // 2. Cek apakah santri ini sudah punya target sebelumnya
    $cek_query = "SELECT id FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
    $cek_result = mysqli_query($conn, $cek_query);

    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0) {
        // SUDAH ADA -> Lakukan UPDATE
        $data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_result);
        $id_target = $data['id'];

        $sql = "UPDATE target_hafalan 
                SET target_ayat_per_hari = $target_ayat, 
                    tanggal_mulai = '$tanggal_mulai', 
                    keterangan = '$keterangan' 
                WHERE id = $id_target";
        
        $pesan = "Target santri berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        // BELUM ADA -> Lakukan INSERT
        $sql = "INSERT INTO target_hafalan (id_santri, tanggal_mulai, target_ayat_per_hari, keterangan) 
                VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal_mulai', $target_ayat, '$keterangan')";
        
        $pesan = "Target santri baru berhasil ditetapkan!";
    }

    // 3. Eksekusi Query
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ $pesan";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menyimpan target: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    // 4. Kembali ke halaman target
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
} else {
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api\santri\store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// ️ UBAH 1: Ambil ID Ustadz yang sedang login
$id_ustadz = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $nama_santri    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_santri']);
    $nis            = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nis']);
    $jk             = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $tgl_lahir      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_lahir']);
    $alamat         = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['alamat']);
    $nama_ortu      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_ortu']);
    $no_hp_ortu     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['no_hp_ortu']);
    $username_ortu  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username_ortu']);
    $password_ortu  = (string) ($_POST['password_ortu'] ?? '');
    if (strlen($password_ortu) < 12) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = 'Password akun orang tua minimal 12 karakter.';
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }
    $id_halaqah     = !empty($_POST['id_halaqah']) ? (int) $_POST['id_halaqah'] : "NULL";

    $check = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_ortu'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($check) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = " Username orang tua '$username_ortu' sudah digunakan!";
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }
    $hash_ortu = password_hash($password_ortu, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

    $sql_user = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) 
                 VALUES ('$username_ortu', '$hash_ortu', '$nama_ortu', 'ortu')";
    
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_user)) {
    
        $id_user_ortu = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

        // ⬅️ UBAH 2: Tambahkan id_ustadz dan id_halaqah di query INSERT
        $sql_santri = "INSERT INTO santri (nama, nis, jenis_kelamin, tanggal_lahir, alamat, nama_ortu, no_hp_ortu, id_user_ortu, id_halaqah, id_ustadz) 
                       VALUES ('$nama_santri', '$nis', '$jk', '$tgl_lahir', '$alamat', '$nama_ortu', '$no_hp_ortu', $id_user_ortu, $id_halaqah, $id_ustadz)";
        
        if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_santri)) {
            $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ Santri '$nama_santri' dan akun Ortu berhasil ditambahkan!";
        } else {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal simpan data santri: " . mysqli_error($conn);
        }
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal membuat akun ortu: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

===== api\santri\delete.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['id'])) {
    $id_santri = (int) $_POST['id'];

   
    $where_binaan = getFilterSantriBinaanSQL($conn, (int) $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'], 's');
    $and_binaan = $where_binaan !== '' ? ' AND ' . preg_replace('/^\s*WHERE\s+/i', '', $where_binaan) : '';
    $query_cari = "SELECT s.id_user_ortu FROM santri s WHERE s.id = $id_santri $and_binaan LIMIT 1";
    $result = mysqli_query($conn, $query_cari);
    $data = $result ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($result) : null;

    if (!$data) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = 'Santri tidak ditemukan atau berada di luar kewenangan Anda.';
        header('Location: kelola_santri.php');
        exit;
    }

    $id_user_ortu = (int) ($data['id_user_ortu'] ?? 0);

 
    if (mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM santri WHERE id = $id_santri")) {
        
    
        if ($id_user_ortu) {
            mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id_user_ortu AND role = 'ortu'");
        }
        
        $_SESSION['success'] = "🗑️ Data santri dan akun ortu berhasil dihapus.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menghapus data santri.";
    }
}

header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
exit;
?>

===== pages\dashboard\index.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$nama = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'];
$role = $_SESSION['role'];

$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];
$chart_murajaah = [];
$stat = ['total_ayat' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0, 'santri_aktif' => 0];
$total_santri = 0;
$total_ustadz = 0;
$predikat_data = ['A' => 0, 'B' => 0, 'C' => 0];
$aktivitas_list = [];
$target_widget = ['total' => 0, 'tercapai' => 0, 'persentase' => 0, 'label' => ''];

if (in_array($role, ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    $total_santri = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM santri"))['total'];
    $total_ustadz = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM users WHERE role = 'ustadz'"))['total'];

    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COUNT(DISTINCT id_santri) as santri_aktif,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                  FROM hafalan 
                  WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q = "SELECT 
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
              FROM hafalan WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $d = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q));
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $d['z'];
        $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $d['m'];
    }

    $predikat_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT predikat, COUNT(*) as jumlah FROM hafalan WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini' GROUP BY predikat");
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($predikat_res)) {
        if (isset($predikat_data[$row['predikat']])) {
            $predikat_data[$row['predikat']] = (int) $row['jumlah'];
        }
    }

    $target_widget['label'] = in_array($role, ['operator', 'pimpinan']) ? 'Santri Mencapai Target Bulanan' : 'Realisasi Target Santri Binaan';
    $target_widget['total'] = $total_santri;
    $capaian_query = "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s.id) as tercapai
                      FROM santri s
                      JOIN target_hafalan t ON s.id = t.id_santri
                      LEFT JOIN (
                          SELECT id_santri, SUM(jumlah_ayat) as total_ayat
                          FROM hafalan 
                          WHERE jenis = 'ziyadah' AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'
                          GROUP BY id_santri
                      ) h ON s.id = h.id_santri
                      WHERE (t.target_ayat_per_hari * 20) <= COALESCE(h.total_ayat, 0)";
    $target_widget['tercapai'] = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $capaian_query))['tercapai'];
    $target_widget['persentase'] = $target_widget['total'] > 0 ? round(($target_widget['tercapai'] / $target_widget['total']) * 100) : 0;

    $aktivitas_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz
                        FROM hafalan h
                        JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id
                        LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                        ORDER BY h.created_at DESC LIMIT 6";
    $aktivitas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $aktivitas_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
    $santri = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu LIMIT 1"));

    if ($santri) {
        $id_santri = $santri['id'];
        $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

        $stat_query = "SELECT 
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                      FROM hafalan 
                      WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
        $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

        for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
            $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
            $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
            $q = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
                  FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
            $d = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q));
            $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $d['z'];
            $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $d['m'];
        }

        $target_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT target_ayat_per_hari FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1");
        $target_anak = ($target_q && mysqli_num_rows($target_q) > 0) ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($target_q) : null;
        $target_ayat_harian = $target_anak ? (int) $target_anak['target_ayat_per_hari'] : 0;
        
        $target_widget['label'] = 'Capaian Target Bulanan Ananda';
        $target_widget['total'] = $target_ayat_harian * 20;
        $target_widget['tercapai'] = (int) $stat['total_ayat'];
        $target_widget['persentase'] = $target_widget['total'] > 0 ? min(100, round(($target_widget['tercapai'] / $target_widget['total']) * 100)) : 0;

        $aktivitas_query = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz
                            FROM hafalan h
                            LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                            WHERE h.id_santri = $id_santri
                            ORDER BY h.created_at DESC LIMIT 6";
        $aktivitas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $aktivitas_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
}
?>
<?php
$page_title = "Dashboard - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus";
include __DIR__ . '/../../includes/header.php';
?>

===== api\pengguna\store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'pimpinan', 'ustadz'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $nama_lengkap = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_lengkap']);
    $username = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username']);
    $password = (string) ($_POST['password'] ?? '');
    if (strlen($password) < 12) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = 'Password akun pengajar minimal 12 karakter.';
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }
    $role = 'ustadz'; 

    $check = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($check) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Username '$username' sudah digunakan! Silakan gunakan username lain.";
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }

    $password_hash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

    $query = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) 
              VALUES ('$username', '$password_hash', '$nama_lengkap', '$role')";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $query)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ Data ustadz/ustadzah berhasil ditambahkan!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Terjadi kesalahan saat menyimpan data.";
    }

    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}
?>

startAppSession();

        $_SESSION['success'] = "🗑️ Data ustadz/ustadzah berhasil dihapus.";

        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menghapus data.";
    }
}

header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");

===== api\rapor\store.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/init_rbac.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['operator', 'ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Pastikan tabel rapor_tahfidz & rapor_tahfidz_detail beserta kolom tambahannya tersedia otomatis
mysqli_query($conn, "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `rapor_tahfidz` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `id_santri` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `kelas` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `judul_materi` varchar(100) DEFAULT 'Materi Tahfidz',
  `semester` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `tahun_ajaran` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `juz` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_ustadz` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4");

mysqli_query($conn, "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `rapor_tahfidz_detail` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `id_rapor` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `urutan` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `nama_materi` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `nilai` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4");

// Tambahkan kolom ekstra khusus Rapor Juz 30 jika belum ada
$extra_cols = [
    "nilai_tahsin" => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "naik_juz"     => "VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT '-'",
    "remedi_juz"   => "VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT '-'",
    "ujian_juz_30" => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_1"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_2"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_3"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "ujian_juz_4"  => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "akhlak"       => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'A'",
    "kerajinan"    => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'B'",
    "kebersihan"   => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'A'",
    "kerapian"     => "VARCHAR(5) DEFAULT 'A'",
    "sakit"        => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "izin"         => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "alfa"         => "INT DEFAULT NULL",
    "catatan_ortu" => "TEXT DEFAULT NULL",
    "tempat_cetak" => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Jalikur Patanangan'",
    "tanggal_cetak"=> "VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL",
    "wali_kelas"   => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL",
    "kepala_stq"   => "VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Ust. Aipda Syaiful Hendra'"
];

foreach ($extra_cols as $col_name => $col_def) {
    $cek_c = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM rapor_tahfidz LIKE '$col_name'");
    if ($cek_c && mysqli_num_rows($cek_c) === 0) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE rapor_tahfidz ADD COLUMN `$col_name` $col_def");
    }
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    if (!santriDalamBinaan($conn, (int) ($_POST['id_santri'] ?? 0), (int) $_SESSION['user_id'], $_SESSION['role'])) {
        http_response_code(403);
        exit('Akses santri ditolak.');
    }
    $id_santri    = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $kelas        = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['kelas']));
    $judul_materi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['judul_materi']));
    $semester     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['semester']));
    $tahun_ajaran = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tahun_ajaran']));
    $juz          = (int) $_POST['juz'];
    $id_ustadz    = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

    // Data tambahan (terutama untuk Rapor Juz 30)
    $nilai_tahsin = ($_POST['nilai_tahsin'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['nilai_tahsin'] : 'NULL';
    $naik_juz     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['naik_juz'] ?? '-'));
    $remedi_juz   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['remedi_juz'] ?? '-'));
    $ujian_30     = ($_POST['ujian_juz_30'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_30'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_1      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_1'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_1'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_2      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_2'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_2'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_3      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_3'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_3'] : 'NULL';
    $ujian_4      = ($_POST['ujian_juz_4'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['ujian_juz_4'] : 'NULL';
    $akhlak       = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['akhlak'] ?? 'A');
    $kerajinan    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['kerajinan'] ?? 'B');
    $kebersihan   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['kebersihan'] ?? 'A');
    $kerapian     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['kerapian'] ?? 'A');
    $sakit        = ($_POST['sakit'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['sakit'] : 'NULL';
    $izin         = ($_POST['izin'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['izin'] : 'NULL';
    $alfa         = ($_POST['alfa'] ?? '') !== '' ? (int)$_POST['alfa'] : 'NULL';
    $catatan_ortu = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['catatan_ortu'] ?? ''));
    $tempat_cetak = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tempat_cetak'] ?? 'Jalikur Patanangan'));
    $tanggal_cetak= mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tanggal_cetak'] ?? date('d F Y')));
    $wali_kelas   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['wali_kelas'] ?? ''));
    $kepala_stq   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['kepala_stq'] ?? 'Ust. Aipda Syaiful Hendra'));

    // Cek apakah Rapor Header untuk santri & juz ini sudah ada
    $cek_query = "SELECT id FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz AND semester = '$semester' AND tahun_ajaran = '$tahun_ajaran'";
    $cek_result = mysqli_query($conn, $cek_query);

    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0) {
        $row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_result);
        $id_rapor = (int) $row['id'];
        $sql_upd = "UPDATE rapor_tahfidz SET 
                        kelas = '$kelas',
                        judul_materi = '$judul_materi',
                        id_ustadz = $id_ustadz,
                        nilai_tahsin = $nilai_tahsin,
                        naik_juz = '$naik_juz',
                        remedi_juz = '$remedi_juz',
                        ujian_juz_30 = $ujian_30,
                        ujian_juz_1 = $ujian_1,
                        ujian_juz_2 = $ujian_2,
                        ujian_juz_3 = $ujian_3,
                        ujian_juz_4 = $ujian_4,
                        akhlak = '$akhlak',
                        kerajinan = '$kerajinan',
                        kebersihan = '$kebersihan',
                        kerapian = '$kerapian',
                        sakit = $sakit,
                        izin = $izin,
                        alfa = $alfa,
                        catatan_ortu = '$catatan_ortu',
                        tempat_cetak = '$tempat_cetak',
                        tanggal_cetak = '$tanggal_cetak',
                        wali_kelas = '$wali_kelas',
                        kepala_stq = '$kepala_stq'
                    WHERE id = $id_rapor";
        mysqli_query($conn, $sql_upd);
        mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = $id_rapor");
    } else {
        $sql_ins = "INSERT INTO rapor_tahfidz (
                        id_santri, kelas, judul_materi, semester, tahun_ajaran, juz, id_ustadz,
                        nilai_tahsin, naik_juz, remedi_juz, ujian_juz_30, ujian_juz_1, ujian_juz_2, ujian_juz_3, ujian_juz_4,
                        akhlak, kerajinan, kebersihan, kerapian, sakit, izin, alfa, catatan_ortu, tempat_cetak, tanggal_cetak, wali_kelas, kepala_stq
                    ) VALUES (
                        $id_santri, '$kelas', '$judul_materi', '$semester', '$tahun_ajaran', $juz, $id_ustadz,
                        $nilai_tahsin, '$naik_juz', '$remedi_juz', $ujian_30, $ujian_1, $ujian_2, $ujian_3, $ujian_4,
                        '$akhlak', '$kerajinan', '$kebersihan', '$kerapian', $sakit, $izin, $alfa, '$catatan_ortu', '$tempat_cetak', '$tanggal_cetak', '$wali_kelas', '$kepala_stq'
                    )";
        mysqli_query($conn, $sql_ins);
        $id_rapor = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    // Simpan Detail Materi & Nilai
    $urutan = 1;
    if (isset($_POST['materi']) && isset($_POST['nilai'])) {
        $materi_arr = $_POST['materi'];
        $nilai_arr  = $_POST['nilai'];

        for ($i = 0; $i < count($materi_arr); $i++) {
            $nama_materi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($materi_arr[$i]));
            $nilai_raw   = trim((string)($nilai_arr[$i] ?? ''));
            $nilai       = ($nilai_raw !== '') ? max(0, min(100, (int)$nilai_raw)) : 'NULL';
            
            if (!empty($nama_materi)) {
                mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO rapor_tahfidz_detail (id_rapor, urutan, nama_materi, nilai) 
                                     VALUES ($id_rapor, $urutan, '$nama_materi', $nilai)");
                $urutan++;
            }
        }
    }

    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data Leger Rapor Juz $juz berhasil disimpan (" . ($urutan - 1) . " baris materi)!";
    header("Location: input_rapor.php?id_santri=$id_santri&juz=$juz&semester=" . urlencode($semester) . "&tahun_ajaran=" . urlencode($tahun_ajaran));
    exit;
}
?>

===== api\auth\login.php =====
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../includes/auth.php';
startAppSession();
require __DIR__ . '/../../config/database.php';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    header('Location: login.php');
    exit;
}

$username = trim((string) ($_POST['username'] ?? ''));
$password = (string) ($_POST['password'] ?? '');
$stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = ? LIMIT 1');
mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 's', $username);
mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
$result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);
$user = $result ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($result) : null;
mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);

if (!$user || !password_verify($password, $user['password'])) {
    $_SESSION['error'] = 'Username atau password salah.';
    header('Location: login.php');
    exit;
}

session_regenerate_id(true);
$_SESSION['user_id'] = $user['id'];
$_SESSION['username'] = $user['username'];
$_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] = $user['nama_lengkap'];
$_SESSION['role'] = $user['role'];
header('Location: dashboard.php');
exit;
